################################################################################
FILE: collect_code.py
################################################################################

from pathlib import Path

# Project root = folder where this script is located
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

# Output TXT file
OUTPUT_FILE = PROJECT_ROOT / "project_code.txt"

# Folders we DON'T want to include
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    ".venv",
    "venv",
    "__pycache__",
    "build",
    "dist",
    "vlc_64",
    "node_modules",
    ".idea",
    ".vscode",
}

# File extensions that are useful source/config files
IMPORTANT_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py",
    ".html",
    ".htm",
    ".css",
    ".js",
    ".json",
    ".lua",
    ".spec",
    ".ini",
    ".cfg",
    ".conf",
    ".txt",
    ".srt",
    ".md",
    ".xml",
    ".yaml",
    ".yml",
}

# Specific important files without relying on extension
IMPORTANT_FILES = {
    ".gitignore",
    "requirements.txt",
    "README",
    "README.md",
    "package.json",
    "package-lock.json",
}


def should_include(path: Path):
    # Skip excluded folders
    for part in path.parts:
        if part in EXCLUDED_DIRS:
            return False

    # Skip our output file
    if path.name == OUTPUT_FILE.name:
        return False

    # Include important filenames
    if path.name in IMPORTANT_FILES:
        return True

    # Include selected source extensions
    if path.suffix.lower() in IMPORTANT_EXTENSIONS:
        return True

    return False


def collect_files():
    files = []

    for path in PROJECT_ROOT.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue

        if should_include(path):
            files.append(path)

    return sorted(files)


def main():
    files = collect_files()

    print(f"Project folder: {PROJECT_ROOT}")
    print(f"Found {len(files)} important files.")
    print()

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as output:

        output.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        output.write("PROJECT SOURCE CODE EXPORT\n")
        output.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        output.write(f"Project: {PROJECT_ROOT.name}\n")
        output.write(f"Files included: {len(files)}\n")
        output.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        for file_path in files:

            relative_path = file_path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)

            print(f"Adding: {relative_path}")

            output.write("\n")
            output.write("#" * 80 + "\n")
            output.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
            output.write("#" * 80 + "\n\n")

            try:
                content = file_path.read_text(
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="replace"
                )

                output.write(content)
                output.write("\n\n")

            except Exception as e:
                output.write(
                    f"[ERROR READING FILE: {e}]\n\n"
                )

    print()
    print("=" * 80)
    print("DONE!")
    print(f"Output file:")
    print(OUTPUT_FILE)
    print("=" * 80)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


################################################################################
FILE: combine.py
################################################################################

import os

# Configuration
OUTPUT_FILE = 'file.txt'

# Add or remove file extensions you consider "important"
ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {'.py', '.html', '.css', '.js', '.iss', '.spec', '.gitignore', '.json', '.md'}

# Folders to completely ignore (build artifacts, envs, deps, assets, etc.)
IGNORED_DIRS = {
    '__pycache__', 'build', 'dist', 'assets', 'Output', 'subtitles',
    'downloads', '.git', '_internal', '.venv', 'venv', 'env',
    'site-packages', 'node_modules', '.idea', '.vscode', 'egg-info'
}

# Exact filenames to skip even if extension matches
IGNORED_FILES = {OUTPUT_FILE, 'combine.py'}

def combine_files():
    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as outfile:

        for root, dirs, files in os.walk('.'):

            # Skip ignored dirs (also catches any dir containing these names, e.g. .venv/Lib/site-packages)
            dirs[:] = [
                d for d in dirs
                if d not in IGNORED_DIRS and not any(ig in os.path.join(root, d) for ig in IGNORED_DIRS)
            ]

            for file in files:
                if file in IGNORED_FILES:
                    continue

                _, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
                ext = ext.lower()

                is_important = (ext in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS) or (file in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS)

                if is_important:
                    filepath = os.path.join(root, file)

                    try:
                        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as infile:
                            content = infile.read()

                        outfile.write(f"{'='*60}\n")
                        outfile.write(f"FILE: {filepath}\n")
                        outfile.write(f"{'='*60}\n\n")
                        outfile.write(content)
                        outfile.write("\n\n")

                        print(f"Added: {filepath}")

                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Skipped {filepath} (Error: {e})")

    print(f"\nDone! All important files have been combined into '{OUTPUT_FILE}'.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    combine_files()


################################################################################
FILE: engines.py
################################################################################

import re
import time
import requests
import subprocess
import os
from deep_translator import GoogleTranslator
from utils import parse_srt, get_ffmpeg_path
import ust_settings as config

# ============================================================
# API TRANSCRIPTION (Groq / OpenAI / Custom)
# ============================================================

def transcribe_audio_chunk(chunk_path, provider, api_key, source_lang=None, custom_url=None, prompt=None):
    if "Groq" in provider:
        url = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/audio/transcriptions"
        model = "whisper-large-v3"
    elif "OpenAI" in provider:
        url = "https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions"
        model = "whisper-1"
    else: # Custom API (OpenAI Format)
        url = custom_url if custom_url else "https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions"
        model = "whisper-1"

    data = {"model": model, "response_format": "verbose_json"}
    if source_lang:
        data["language"] = source_lang
    if prompt and prompt.strip():
        data["prompt"] = prompt.strip()

    max_retries = 4
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            with open(chunk_path, "rb") as f:
                response = requests.post(
                    url, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"},
                    files={"file": (os.path.basename(chunk_path), f, "audio/mp4")},
                    data=data, timeout=300
                )

            if response.status_code == 200:
                return response.json()
            elif response.status_code == 429:
                # Rate limited — wait with exponential backoff and retry
                wait_time = min(30, (2 ** attempt) * 5)
                if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                    time.sleep(wait_time)
                    continue
                else:
                    raise Exception(
                        f"{provider} API Rate Limit: You've hit the usage quota.\n\n"
                        "💡 Solutions:\n"
                        "• Wait a few minutes and try again\n"
                        "• Check your API provider plan & billing"
                    )
            elif response.status_code == 401:
                raise Exception(f"{provider} API Error: Invalid API Key. Please verify your credentials.")
            else:
                raise Exception(f"API Error {response.status_code}:\n{response.text[:400]}")

        except requests.exceptions.Timeout:
            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                time.sleep(3)
                continue
            raise Exception(f"{provider} API timed out after multiple attempts.")
        except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:
            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                time.sleep(3)
                continue
            raise Exception("Network connection error. Check your internet connection or URL.")

    raise Exception(f"Failed to transcribe after {max_retries} attempts.")

# ============================================================
# TRANSLATION — with batching and exponential backoff
# ============================================================

def split_text_for_translation(text, max_chars=3000):
    if len(text) <= max_chars: return [text]
    sentences = re.split(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+', text)
    chunks, current = [], ""
    for sentence in sentences:
        if len(current) + len(sentence) + 1 <= max_chars:
            current = (current + " " + sentence).strip()
        else:
            if current: chunks.append(current)
            if len(sentence) > max_chars:
                for i in range(0, len(sentence), max_chars):
                    chunks.append(sentence[i:i + max_chars])
                current = ""
            else:
                current = sentence
    if current: chunks.append(current)
    return chunks if chunks else [text]

def translate_single_chunk(text, target_code, romanize=False):
    max_retries = 4
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            if not romanize:
                res = GoogleTranslator(source='auto', target=target_code).translate(text)
                if res: return res
            else:
                url    = "https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single"
                params = {"client": "gtx", "sl": "auto", "tl": target_code,
                          "dt": ["t", "rm"], "q": text}
                resp   = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=15)
                if resp.status_code == 200:
                    data     = resp.json()
                    romanized = "".join(p[2] for p in data[0] if len(p) > 2 and p[2])
                    normal    = "".join(p[0] for p in data[0] if p[0])
                    return romanized.strip() or normal.strip() or text
                elif resp.status_code == 429:
                    wait_time = min(30, (2 ** attempt) * 2)
                    time.sleep(wait_time)
                    continue
        except Exception:
            wait_time = min(15, (2 ** attempt) * 1.5)
            time.sleep(wait_time)
    return text

def translate_text(eng_text, target_code, romanize=False):
    if target_code == "en" or not eng_text.strip():
        return eng_text
    chunks = split_text_for_translation(eng_text, config.MAX_CHARS_PER_CHUNK)
    return " ".join(translate_single_chunk(c, target_code, romanize) for c in chunks)

def translate_batch(segments, target_code, romanize=False, batch_size=None):
    if target_code == "en":
        return segments

    if batch_size is None:
        batch_size = config.TRANSLATION_BATCH_SIZE

    SEPARATOR = " ||| "
    total = len(segments)
    
    import concurrent.futures

    def process_batch(batch_start):
        batch_end = min(batch_start + batch_size, total)
        batch = segments[batch_start:batch_end]
        combined = SEPARATOR.join(seg["text"] for seg in batch)
        translated = translate_single_chunk(combined, target_code, romanize)
        
        result_batch = []
        if translated and SEPARATOR in translated:
            parts = translated.split(SEPARATOR)
            if len(parts) == len(batch):
                for i, part in enumerate(parts):
                    result_batch.append((batch_start + i, part.strip()))
                return result_batch
                
        for i, seg in enumerate(batch):
            result_batch.append((batch_start + i, translate_single_chunk(seg["text"], target_code, romanize)))
        return result_batch

    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=config.MAX_TRANSLATION_WORKERS) as executor:
        futures = [executor.submit(process_batch, start) for start in range(0, total, batch_size)]
        for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):
            for idx, text in future.result():
                segments[idx]["text"] = text

    return segments


# ============================================================
# SYNC DETECTION
# ============================================================

def detect_sync_offset(video_path, srt_path, status_callback=None):
    segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
    if not segments: return None, "No subtitles found in file."

    first_sub_time = segments[0]['start']
    if status_callback:
        status_callback("Analyzing audio for auto-sync (may take ~30s)...")

    try:
        cf  = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
        ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
        
        cmd = [
            ffmpeg_path, "-i", video_path, "-t", "300",
            "-af", "silencedetect=noise=-35dB:d=0.3", "-f", "null", "-"
        ]
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                    creationflags=cf, timeout=120)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return None, "ffmpeg.exe is missing! Please make sure it is properly bundled with the application."
            
        stderr = result.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')

        silence_ends = [float(x) for x in re.findall(r'silence_end: ([\d.]+)', stderr)]
        first_speech = silence_ends[0] if silence_ends else 0.0

        offset = round(first_speech - first_sub_time, 2)
        info   = (f"First speech at {first_speech:.2f}s  |  "
                  f"First subtitle at {first_sub_time:.2f}s  |  "
                  f"Suggested offset: {offset:+.2f}s")
        return offset, info
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return None, "Audio analysis timed out."
    except Exception as e:
        return None, f"Auto-detect failed: {e}"


# ============================================================
# ONLINE SUBTITLE FETCHING
# ============================================================

def fetch_online_subtitle(video_path, target_lang="en", status_callback=None):
    try:
        from subliminal import download_best_subtitles, region, scan_video
        from babelfish import Language
    except ImportError:
        raise Exception("Subliminal is not installed. Run 'pip install subliminal'")
        
    if status_callback: status_callback("Scanning video hash...")
    
    cache_dir = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cache", "universalsubtitles", "subliminal")
    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        region.configure('dogpile.cache.dbm', arguments={'filename': os.path.join(cache_dir, 'cache.dbm')})
    except Exception:
        pass  
    
    video = scan_video(video_path)
    if not video:
        raise Exception("Could not parse video metadata for hash.")
        
    if status_callback: status_callback(f"Searching for '{target_lang}' subtitles online...")
    
    try:
        lang = Language.fromalpha2(target_lang)
    except Exception:
        try:
            lang = Language(target_lang)
        except Exception:
            lang = Language('eng')  
    
    langs = {lang}
    subtitles = download_best_subtitles([video], langs)
    
    if video in subtitles and subtitles[video]:
        best_sub = subtitles[video][0]
        if status_callback: status_callback("Saving subtitle...")
        
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]
        out_path = os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_{target_lang}_online.srt")
        
        content = best_sub.content
        if content:
            if isinstance(content, bytes):
                content = content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            with open(out_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(content)
            if status_callback: status_callback(f"✅ Subtitle downloaded instantly!")
            return out_path
        
        from subliminal.core import save_subtitles
        save_subtitles(video, [best_sub], single=True, directory=config.SUB_DIR, encoding='utf-8')
        
        video_base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video.name))[0]
        for candidate in [
            os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{video_base}.srt"),
            os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{video_base}.{target_lang}.srt"),
        ]:
            if os.path.exists(candidate):
                import shutil
                shutil.move(candidate, out_path)
                if status_callback: status_callback(f"✅ Subtitle downloaded instantly!")
                return out_path
        return None
    else:
        raise Exception(f"No '{target_lang}' subtitle found online for this video.\nTip: Subtitles are matched by file hash — try a well-known movie or TV show.")

FILE: .\engines.py

import re
import time
import requests
import subprocess
import os
from deep_translator import GoogleTranslator
from utils import parse_srt, get_ffmpeg_path

# ============================================================
# API TRANSCRIPTION (Groq / OpenAI / Custom)
# ============================================================

                raise Exception(f"{provider} API Error: Invalid API Key. Please verify your credentials.")

# ============================================================
# ONLINE SUBTITLE FETCHING
# ============================================================

def fetch_online_subtitle(video_path, target_lang="en", status_callback=None):
    try:
        from subliminal import download_best_subtitles, region, scan_video
        from babelfish import Language
    except ImportError:
        raise Exception("Subliminal is not installed. Run 'pip install subliminal'")
        
    if status_callback: status_callback("Scanning video hash...")
    
    cache_dir = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cache", "universalsubtitles", "subliminal")
    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        region.configure('dogpile.cache.dbm', arguments={'filename': os.path.join(cache_dir, 'cache.dbm')})
    except Exception:
        pass  
    
    video = scan_video(video_path)
    if not video:
        raise Exception("Could not parse video metadata for hash.")
        
    if status_callback: status_callback(f"Searching for '{target_lang}' subtitles online...")
    
    try:
        lang = Language.fromalpha2(target_lang)
    except Exception:
        try:
            lang = Language(target_lang)
        except Exception:
            lang = Language('eng')  
    
    langs = {lang}
    subtitles = download_best_subtitles([video], langs)
    
    if video in subtitles and subtitles[video]:
        best_sub = subtitles[video][0]
        if status_callback: status_callback("Saving subtitle...")
        
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]
        out_path = os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_{target_lang}_online.srt")
        
        content = best_sub.content
        if content:
            if isinstance(content, bytes):
                content = content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            with open(out_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(content)
            if status_callback: status_callback(f"✅ Subtitle downloaded instantly!")
            return out_path
        
        from subliminal.core import save_subtitles
        save_subtitles(video, [best_sub], single=True, directory=config.SUB_DIR, encoding='utf-8')
        
        video_base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video.name))[0]
        for candidate in [
            os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{video_base}.srt"),
            os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{video_base}.{target_lang}.srt"),
        ]:
            if os.path.exists(candidate):
                import shutil
                shutil.move(candidate, out_path)
                if status_callback: status_callback(f"✅ Subtitle downloaded instantly!")
                return out_path
        return None
    else:
        raise Exception(f"No '{target_lang}' subtitle found online for this video.\nTip: Subtitles are matched by file hash — try a well-known movie or TV show.")

import os
import warnings
import multiprocessing

if __name__ == '__main__':
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()

warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
os.environ["PYTHONWARNINGS"] = "ignore"

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, ttk, colorchooser
import threading
import subprocess
import sys
import re

import ust_settings as config
from utils import clean_path, format_timestamp, parse_srt, get_available_subtitles, detect_paired_file, generate_ass_content, resource_path, get_ffmpeg_path

# Cleaned up imports (Removed offline references)
from engines import transcribe_audio_chunk, translate_batch, detect_sync_offset
from vlc_integration import launch_vlc_preview, apply_subtitle_to_vlc, install_vlc_extension, remove_vlc_extension, get_vlc_ext_path
from ui_windows import show_srt_preview, show_style_preview, open_history
from security import save_api_key, load_api_key
from reframing import reframe_video

# ============================================================
# HELPER / CALLBACKS
# ============================================================
def update_status(text):
    if app and status_label:
        app.after(0, lambda: status_label.config(text=f"Status: {text}"))

def update_progress(done, total, label=""):
    pct = int((done / total) * 100) if total > 0 else 0
    if app and progress_var:  app.after(0, lambda: progress_var.set(pct))
    if label and app and progress_label:
        app.after(0, lambda: progress_label.config(text=f"{done}/{total} {label}"))

def populate_all_tabs(srt_path):
    app.after(0, lambda: trans_srt_var.set(srt_path))
    app.after(0, lambda: sync_srt_var.set(srt_path))
    app.after(0, lambda: custom_srt_var.set(srt_path))


# ============================================================
# WORKFLOW LOGIC
# ============================================================
def run_generation():
    video_path       = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_lang      = gen_lang_var.get()
    api_key          = api_key_var.get().strip()
    provider         = provider_var.get()
    custom_url       = custom_url_var.get().strip()
    romanize         = gen_romanize_var.get()
    source_lang_name = gen_source_lang_var.get()
    source_lang_code = config.WHISPER_LANGUAGES.get(source_lang_name)

    if not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a video file!")
    if not os.path.exists(video_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Video file not found!")
    if not api_key: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please enter your API Key!")
    save_api_key(api_key)

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: gen_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            if os.path.exists(config.TEMP_DIR):
                for fn in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR):
                    try: os.remove(os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, fn))
                    except: pass

            update_status("Compressing audio for AI (Wait ~15s)...")
            chunk_pattern = os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, "chunk_%03d.m4a")
            cf  = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
            
            ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
            segment_time = str(config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME)
            cmd = [ffmpeg_path, "-y", "-i", video_path, "-vn", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "64k",
                   "-ar", "16000", "-ac", "1", "-af", "loudnorm", "-f", "segment",
                   "-segment_time", segment_time, chunk_pattern]

            try:
                proc = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, creationflags=cf)
                if proc.returncode != 0:
                    raise Exception(f"FFmpeg failed. Is the file a valid video?\n\n{proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')[-400:]}")
            except FileNotFoundError:
                raise Exception("ffmpeg.exe not found! Please make sure the app was installed correctly.")

            chunks = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR) if f.endswith('.m4a')])
            if not chunks:
                raise Exception("No audio chunks extracted — the video may have no audio track.")

            all_segments = []
            for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
                lang_info = f" ({source_lang_name})" if source_lang_code else " (Auto-Detect)"

                # AI Transcription
                update_status(f"☁️ Transcribing Part {i+1}/{len(chunks)}{lang_info} via {provider}...")
                update_progress(i, len(chunks), "parts")
                
                ai_data = transcribe_audio_chunk(
                    os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, chunk), 
                    provider, 
                    api_key, 
                    source_lang_code,
                    custom_url
                )

                offset = i * config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME
                for seg in ai_data.get("segments", []):
                    text = seg["text"].strip()
                    if text:
                        all_segments.append({"start": seg["start"] + offset, "end": seg["end"] + offset, "text": text})

            if not all_segments: raise Exception("No speech detected in this video. Please ensure the video contains spoken words.")

            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            if target_code != "en":
                mode = "Manglish/Hinglish" if romanize else "Native Script"
                update_status(f"🌐 Batch translating {len(all_segments)} lines ({mode})...")
                update_progress(0, 100, "%")
                translate_batch(all_segments, target_code, romanize)
                update_progress(100, 100, "%")

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(all_segments) if s['text'])
            suffix   = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
            srt_name = f"{os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]}{suffix}.srt"
            srt_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, srt_name))

            with open(srt_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
            for fn in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR):
                try: os.remove(os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, fn))
                except: pass

            update_status(f"✅ Done! {len(all_segments)} subtitle lines generated.")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(srt_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, srt_path, f"✅ Subtitles Generated!\n{len(all_segments)} lines | {target_lang}", video_path)

        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Generation Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: gen_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()


def run_online_search():
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_lang = gen_lang_var.get()
    
    if not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a video file!")
    if not os.path.exists(video_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Video file not found!")
    
    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: fast_dl_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            from engines import fetch_online_subtitle
            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            out_path = fetch_online_subtitle(video_path, target_code, update_status)
            if out_path:
                populate_all_tabs(out_path)
                apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, out_path, f"✅ Online Subtitle Downloaded!\n{target_lang}", video_path)
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Search Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: fast_dl_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
            
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_translation():
    srt_path    = clean_path(trans_srt_var.get().strip())
    target_lang = trans_lang_var.get()
    romanize    = trans_romanize_var.get()
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Valid SRT file not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: trans_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Parsing SRT file...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            if not segments: raise Exception("Could not parse the SRT file.")

            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            errors = 0
            if target_code != "en":
                mode = "Manglish/Hinglish" if romanize else "Native Script"
                update_status(f"🌐 Batch translating {len(segments)} lines to {target_lang} ({mode})...")
                update_progress(0, 100, "%")
                translate_batch(segments, target_code, romanize)
                update_progress(100, 100, "%")

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(segments))
            base      = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
            suffix    = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
            save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}{suffix}.srt"))

            with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
            note = f"\n⚠️ {errors} errors" if errors else ""
            update_status(f"✅ Translation Done!{note}")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(save_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, save_path, f"✅ Translation Finished!\n{len(segments)} lines → {target_lang}{note}", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Translation Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: trans_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_reframe():
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_ratio = reframe_ratio_var.get()
    mode = reframe_mode_var.get()

    if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
        return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a valid video file!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: reframe_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED, text="Reframing..."))
            update_status(f"✂️ Reframing video to {target_ratio} ({mode})...")
            update_progress(0, 100, "Reframing")
            
            out_path = reframe_video(video_path, target_ratio, mode)
            
            update_status(f"✅ Reframe Done! Saved to {os.path.basename(out_path)}")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showinfo("Reframing Complete", f"Saved successfully to:\n{out_path}"))
            
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Reframing Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: reframe_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL, text="✂️ Reframe Video"))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_auto_sync():
    srt_path   = clean_path(sync_srt_var.get().strip())
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    if not srt_path or not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Select Video in Tab 1 and an SRT file here!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: auto_sync_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED, text="Analyzing..."))
            offset, info = detect_sync_offset(video_path, srt_path, update_status)
            if offset is None:
                app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Auto-Sync Failed", info))
                update_status("Auto-sync failed.")
                return
            offset_str = f"{offset:+.2f}"
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_offset_var.set(offset_str))
            update_status(f"✅ Auto-detected offset: {offset_str}s")
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showinfo("🎯 Auto-Sync Result", f"Detected Offset: {offset_str}s\n\n{info}\nClick 'SYNC & APPLY' to save!"))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: auto_sync_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL, text="🔍 Auto Detect Offset"))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_sync():
    srt_path = clean_path(sync_srt_var.get().strip())
    try: offset_val = float(sync_offset_var.get().strip().replace('+', ''))
    except ValueError: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Offset must be a number")
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "SRT file not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Adjusting SRT timestamps...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            for seg in segments:
                seg['start'] = max(0, seg['start'] + offset_val)
                seg['end']   = max(0.001, seg['end'] + offset_val)
                if seg['end'] <= seg['start']: seg['end'] = seg['start'] + 0.5

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(segments))
            base      = re.sub(r'_synced$', '', os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0])
            save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_synced.srt"))
            with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)

            update_status(f"✅ Sync done! Offset: {offset_val:+.2f}s")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(save_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, save_path, f"✅ Sync Applied!\nOffset: {offset_val:+.2f} seconds", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Sync Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def pick_color(var, btn):
    initial = var.get() if var.get().startswith('#') else '#ffffff'
    result  = colorchooser.askcolor(color=initial, title="Pick Color")
    if result and result[1]:
        var.set(result[1])
        btn.config(bg=result[1], fg="white" if result[1].lower() in ['#000000','#111111','#222222','#333333'] else "black")

def run_export_ass():
    srt_path = clean_path(custom_srt_var.get().strip())
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "File not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: export_ass_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Generating styled ASS subtitle...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            if not segments: raise Exception("Could not parse SRT file.")

            style = {
                "font_name": custom_font_var.get() or "Arial",
                "font_size": custom_size_var.get(),
                "primary_color": custom_text_color_var.get(),
                "outline_color": custom_outline_color_var.get(),
                "background": custom_bg_var.get(),
                "bold": custom_bold_var.get(),
                "italic": custom_italic_var.get(),
                "outline": custom_outline_sz_var.get(),
                "shadow": custom_shadow_var.get(),
                "alignment": 8 if custom_position_var.get() == "top" else 2,
            }
            ass_content = generate_ass_content(segments, style)
            base        = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
            ass_path    = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_styled.ass"))
            with open(ass_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(ass_content)

            update_status("✅ Styled ASS subtitle exported!")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, ass_path, "✅ Styled Subtitle Exported as .ASS", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Export Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: export_ass_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def on_video_change(*args):
    v_path = gen_video_var.get().strip()
    subs   = get_available_subtitles(v_path)
    if trans_srt_cb:  trans_srt_cb['values']  = subs
    if sync_srt_cb:   sync_srt_cb['values']   = subs
    if custom_srt_cb: custom_srt_cb['values'] = subs
    best = detect_paired_file(v_path, "srt")
    if best: populate_all_tabs(best)
    elif subs:
        if not trans_srt_var.get(): populate_all_tabs(subs[0])

def browse_srt(var):
    f = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("Subtitles", "*.srt *.ass")])
    if f: var.set(clean_path(f))

def browse_video(var):
    f = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("Video", "*.mp4 *.mkv *.avi *.ts *.mov *.wmv")])
    if f: var.set(clean_path(f))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # UI SETUP
    app = tk.Tk()
    app.title("Universal Subtitles Toolkit (VLC Edition)")
    app.geometry("560x720")
    app.configure(bg=config.DARK_BG)
    app.resizable(False, False)

    try: app.iconbitmap(resource_path("logo.ico"))
    except: pass

    style = ttk.Style()
    style.theme_use('clam')
    style.configure('TCombobox', fieldbackground="white", background="white", foreground="black", borderwidth=0)
    style.map('TCombobox', fieldbackground=[('readonly', 'white'), ('focus', 'white')],
              foreground=[('readonly', 'black'), ('focus', 'black')],
              selectbackground=[('readonly', config.VLC_ORANGE)], selectforeground=[('readonly', 'black')])
    style.configure("Orange.Horizontal.TProgressbar", background=config.VLC_ORANGE, troughcolor=config.PANEL_BG, bordercolor=config.DARK_BG, lightcolor=config.VLC_ORANGE, darkcolor=config.VLC_ORANGE)

    provider_var      = tk.StringVar(value="Groq (Lightning Fast)")
    api_key_var       = tk.StringVar(value=load_api_key())
    custom_url_var    = tk.StringVar(value="https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions")

    gen_video_var     = tk.StringVar()
    gen_lang_var      = tk.StringVar(value="English")
    gen_romanize_var  = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    gen_source_lang_var = tk.StringVar(value="Auto Detect (Recommended)")
    trans_srt_var     = tk.StringVar()
    trans_lang_var    = tk.StringVar(value="Malayalam")
    trans_romanize_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    sync_srt_var      = tk.StringVar()
    sync_offset_var   = tk.StringVar(value="+0.0")
    custom_srt_var        = tk.StringVar()
    custom_font_var       = tk.StringVar(value="Arial")
    custom_size_var       = tk.IntVar(value=20)

    def preview_ass_in_vlc():
        srt_path = clean_path(custom_srt_var.get().strip())
        if not srt_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "No file selected!")
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
        ass_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_styled.ass"))
        if os.path.exists(ass_path):
            launch_vlc_preview(clean_path(gen_video_var.get()), ass_path)
        else:
            messagebox.showerror("Not Found", "Please click '🎨 EXPORT' first to generate the styled .ass file before previewing.")

    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        passed = sys.argv[1]

        if passed.startswith("universalsubtitles://") or (len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[1] == "--uri"):
            from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs, unquote
            uri = passed if passed.startswith("universalsubtitles://") else sys.argv[2]
            uri = uri.strip('"').rstrip('/')
            parsed = urlparse(uri)
            params = parse_qs(parsed.query)
            video = unquote(params.get("video", [""])[0])
            if video and os.path.isfile(video):
                gen_video_var.set(clean_path(video))
        else:
            passed = clean_path(passed)
            if os.path.isfile(passed):
                if passed.lower().endswith('.srt'):
                    populate_all_tabs(passed)
                    vm = detect_paired_file(passed, "video")
                    if vm: gen_video_var.set(vm)
                else: gen_video_var.set(passed)

    show_tab(0)
    app.mainloop()

if os.path.exists(vlc_dir):
    os.environ['PYTHON_VLC_MODULE_PATH'] = vlc_dir
    if hasattr(os, 'add_dll_directory'):
        os.add_dll_directory(vlc_dir)

                               QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, 
                               QLineEdit, QCheckBox, QStackedWidget, QProgressBar,
                               QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QFrame, QScrollArea, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem)

class HistoryListWidget(QListWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setDragEnabled(True)

    def startDrag(self, supportedActions):
        item = self.currentItem()
        if not item: return
        path = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        if not path or not os.path.exists(path): return
        
        mime_data = QMimeData()
        mime_data.setUrls([QUrl.fromLocalFile(path)])
        drag = QDrag(self)
        drag.setMimeData(mime_data)
        drag.exec(supportedActions)

    def keyPressEvent(self, event):
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLineEdit, QTextEdit
        focus_widget = QApplication.focusWidget()
        if isinstance(focus_widget, (QLineEdit, QTextEdit)):
            super().keyPressEvent(event)
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key_Space:
            self.toggle_play()
        else:
            super().keyPressEvent(event)

    def run_fast_download(self):
        video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "No Video", "Please load a video first using the AI Generate tab or drag a video onto the player.")
            return

        lang_name = self.ws_lang.currentText()
        lang_code = self.ws_lang_map.get(lang_name, "eng")

        self.ws_fast_btn.setEnabled(False)
        self.ws_results_list.clear()
        self.ws_status_lbl.setText("🔍 Searching subtitle databases...")
        self._ws_results_data = []

        def _task():
            try:
                from subliminal import list_subtitles, download_subtitles, Video
                from babelfish import Language
                import pysubs2

                video = Video.fromname(os.path.basename(video_path))
                langs = {Language(lang_code)}

                # Search all providers
                subs_found = list_subtitles({video}, langs)
                subs = subs_found.get(video, [])

                if not subs:
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update("❌ No subtitles found online. Try a different language.", enable=True))
                    return

                # Show results list
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_populate_list(subs, video_path, lang_code))

                # Auto-download the first (best) result
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update(f"⬇️ Downloading best result (1 of {len(subs)})..."))
                best = subs[0]
                download_subtitles([best])

                if best.content:
                    video_dir = os.path.dirname(video_path)
                    base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]
                    out_path = os.path.join(video_dir, f"{base}.{lang_code}.srt")
                    subs_obj = pysubs2.SSAFile.from_string(best.content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
                    subs_obj.save(out_path)
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_load_downloaded(out_path, len(subs)))
                else:
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update("❌ Download failed — subtitle content empty. Try another from the list.", enable=True))
            except Exception as e:
                err = str(e)
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update(f"❌ Error: {err}", enable=True))

        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def _ws_update(self, msg, enable=False):
        self.ws_status_lbl.setText(msg)
        if enable:
            self.ws_fast_btn.setEnabled(True)

    def _ws_populate_list(self, subs, video_path, lang_code):
        self._ws_results_data = [(s, video_path, lang_code) for s in subs]
        self.ws_results_list.clear()
        for i, s in enumerate(subs):
            provider = type(s).__name__.replace("Subtitle", "").replace("Com", ".com")
            title = (getattr(s, 'title', None) or getattr(s, 'release', None)
                     or getattr(s, 'filename', None) or str(getattr(s, 'subtitle_id', '')))
            star = "⭐ " if i == 0 else "   "
            item = QListWidgetItem(f"{star}[{provider}] {title or f'Result #{i+1}'}")
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, i)
            self.ws_results_list.addItem(item)

    def download_web_sub(self, item):
        if not item: return
        idx = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        if not hasattr(self, '_ws_results_data') or idx >= len(self._ws_results_data):
            return

        sub, video_path, lang_code = self._ws_results_data[idx]
        self.ws_status_lbl.setText("⬇️ Downloading selected subtitle...")
        self.ws_fast_btn.setEnabled(False)

        def _task():
            try:
                from subliminal import download_subtitles
                import pysubs2

                download_subtitles([sub])
                if sub.content:
                    video_dir = os.path.dirname(video_path)
                    base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]
                    out_path = os.path.join(video_dir, f"{base}.{lang_code}.srt")
                    subs_obj = pysubs2.SSAFile.from_string(sub.content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
                    subs_obj.save(out_path)
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_load_downloaded(out_path, None))
                else:
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update("❌ Empty content. Try another result.", enable=True))
            except Exception as e:
                err = str(e)
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self._ws_update(f"❌ Error: {err}", enable=True))

        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def _ws_load_downloaded(self, path, total):
        msg = f"✅ Downloaded & loaded! ({total} results available above)" if total else "✅ Downloaded & loaded!"
        self.ws_status_lbl.setText(msg)
        self.ws_fast_btn.setEnabled(True)
        self.populate_all_tabs(path)
        self.refresh_history_list()



    # --- UI Helpers ---

    def browse_video(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select Video", "", "Video Files (*.mp4 *.mkv *.avi *.ts *.mov)")
        if path:
            self.load_dropped_video(path)

    def load_dropped_video(self, path):
        if not path: return
        self.gen_video_path.setText(clean_path(path))
        self.player.play(clean_path(path))
        
        self.refresh_history_list(clean_path(path))
        
        subs = get_available_subtitles(clean_path(path))
        best = detect_paired_file(clean_path(path), "srt")
        if best:
            self.populate_all_tabs(best)
        elif subs:
            self.populate_all_tabs(subs[0])

    def browse_srt(self, line_edit):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select Subtitle", "", "Subtitle Files (*.srt *.ass)")
        if path:
            line_edit.setText(clean_path(path))
            self.populate_all_tabs(clean_path(path))
            
    def browse_standalone_srt(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select Subtitle", "", "Subtitle Files (*.srt *.ass)")
        if path:
            self.populate_all_tabs(clean_path(path))

    def refresh_history_list(self, video_path=None):
        self.history_list.clear()
        if not video_path:
            video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        subs = get_available_subtitles(video_path)
        for sub in subs:
            item = QListWidgetItem(os.path.basename(sub))
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, sub)
            self.history_list.addItem(item)
            
    def load_history_item(self, item):
        sub_path = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        if sub_path and os.path.exists(sub_path):
            self.populate_all_tabs(sub_path)

    def populate_all_tabs(self, srt_path):
        self.trans_srt_path.setText(srt_path)
        self.sync_srt_path.setText(srt_path)
        self.cust_srt_path.setText(srt_path)
        
        # Load segments into timeline
        try:
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            self.timeline.set_segments(segments)
            self.timeline.srt_path = srt_path  # Track file for saving double-click edits
            # Store the pending subtitle; apply immediately if playing, else on next play
            self._pending_subtitle = srt_path
            if self.player.media_player.is_playing():
                self.player.set_subtitle(srt_path)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Failed to parse SRT for timeline:", e)

    def toggle_play(self):
        if self.player.media_player.is_playing():
            self.player.pause()
            self.play_btn.setText("▶ Play")
        else:
            self.player.media_player.play()
            self.play_btn.setText("⏸ Pause")

    def update_timeline(self):
        t = self.player.get_time()
        if t >= 0:
            dur = self.player.get_length()
            if dur > 0:
                self.timeline.set_duration(dur)
            self.timeline.set_current_time(t)

            # If playing just started and we have a pending subtitle, apply it
            if self.player.media_player.is_playing():
                pending = getattr(self, '_pending_subtitle', None)
                if pending:
                    self._pending_subtitle = None
                    self.player.set_subtitle(pending, force=True)

            is_playing = self.player.media_player.is_playing()
            self.play_btn.setText("⏸ Pause" if is_playing else "▶ Play")

    def set_status(self, text):
        self.status_bar_lbl.setText(f"Status: {text}")

    def set_progress(self, done, total, label):
        pct = int((done / total) * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        self.progress_bar.setValue(pct)
        self.progress_lbl.setText(f"{done}/{total} {label}")
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
        self.progress_lbl.setVisible(True)

    def show_error(self, err):
        QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", err)

    # --- Worker Methods ---
    def run_generation(self):
        if getattr(self, 'is_generating', False):
            if hasattr(self, 'current_task_token'):
                self.current_task_token["cancelled"] = True
                
            if hasattr(self, 'ffmpeg_proc') and self.ffmpeg_proc:
                try: self.ffmpeg_proc.kill()
                except: pass
                
            # Instantly reset the UI so user doesn't wait for the blocking HTTP request
            self.is_generating = False
            self.pulse_timer.stop()
            self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("")
            self.gen_btn.setText("⚡ Generate Subtitles")
            self.gen_btn.setEnabled(True)
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
            self.progress_lbl.setVisible(False)
            self.set_status("Generation Cancelled.")
            return

        video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        target_lang = self.gen_target_lang.currentText()
        api_key = self.gen_api.text().strip()
        provider = self.gen_provider.currentText()
        romanize = self.gen_romanize.isChecked()
        source_lang_name = self.gen_src_lang.currentText()
        source_lang_code = config.WHISPER_LANGUAGES.get(source_lang_name)

        if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
            self.show_error("Please select a valid video file!")
            return
        if not api_key:
            self.show_error("Please enter your API Key!")
            return

        save_api_key(api_key)
        
        self.is_generating = True
        token = {"cancelled": False}
        self.current_task_token = token
        
        self.gen_btn.setText("🛑 Cancel Generation")
        self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #d9534f; color: white;")
        
        self.pulse_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.pulse_state = False
        def pulse():
            self.pulse_state = not self.pulse_state
            if self.pulse_state:
                self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ff3333; color: white;")
            else:
                self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #cc0000; color: white;")
        self.pulse_timer.timeout.connect(pulse)
        self.pulse_timer.start(500)

        def _task():
            success = False
            try:
                if os.path.exists(config.TEMP_DIR):
                    for fn in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR):
                        try: os.remove(os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, fn))
                        except: pass

                self.signals.update_status.emit("Compressing audio for AI (Wait ~15s)...")
                chunk_pattern = os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, "chunk_%03d.m4a")
                cf = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
                ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
                segment_time = str(config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME)
                cmd = [ffmpeg_path, "-y", "-i", video_path, "-vn", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "64k",
                       "-ar", "16000", "-ac", "1", "-af", "loudnorm", "-f", "segment",
                       "-segment_time", segment_time, chunk_pattern]

                self.ffmpeg_proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, creationflags=cf)
                proc_out, proc_err = self.ffmpeg_proc.communicate()
                
                if token["cancelled"]:
                    raise Exception("Generation cancelled by user.")
                if self.ffmpeg_proc.returncode != 0:
                    raise Exception(f"FFmpeg failed. {proc_err.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')[-400:]}")

                chunks = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR) if f.endswith('.m4a')])
                if not chunks: raise Exception("No audio chunks extracted.")

                all_segments = []
                for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
                    if token["cancelled"]:
                        raise Exception("Generation cancelled by user.")
                        
                    self.signals.update_status.emit(f"☁️ Transcribing Part {i+1}/{len(chunks)}...")
                    self.signals.update_progress.emit(i, len(chunks), "parts")
                    
                    ai_data = transcribe_audio_chunk(
                        os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, chunk), provider, api_key, source_lang_code, "https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions"
                    )
                    offset = i * config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME
                    for seg in ai_data.get("segments", []):
                        text = seg["text"].strip()
                        if text:
                            all_segments.append({"start": seg["start"] + offset, "end": seg["end"] + offset, "text": text})

                if not all_segments: raise Exception("No speech detected.")

                target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
                if target_code != "en":
                    if token["cancelled"]: raise Exception("Generation cancelled by user.")
                    self.signals.update_status.emit(f"🌐 Translating {len(all_segments)} lines...")
                    translate_batch(all_segments, target_code, romanize)

                srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                      for i, s in enumerate(all_segments) if s['text'])
                suffix = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
                srt_name = f"{os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]}{suffix}.srt"
                srt_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, srt_name))

                with open(srt_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
                
                self.signals.update_status.emit(f"✅ Done! {len(all_segments)} lines generated.")
                self.signals.update_progress.emit(100, 100, "%")
                self.signals.populate_tabs.emit(srt_path)
                # Refresh history list to show the new subtitle
                QTimer.singleShot(0, self.refresh_history_list)
                success = True
            except Exception as e:
                if str(e) == "Generation cancelled by user.":
                    pass # Handled by the main thread UI reset
                else:
                    self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
                    self.signals.update_status.emit("Error.")
            finally:
                if not token["cancelled"]:
                    self.is_generating = False
                    self.ffmpeg_proc = None
                    def reset_btn():
                        self.pulse_timer.stop()
                        if success:
                            self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #28a745; color: white;")
                            self.gen_btn.setText("✅ Successfully Generated!")
                            
                            def revert_default():
                                self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("")
                                self.gen_btn.setText("⚡ Generate Subtitles")
                                self.gen_btn.setEnabled(True)
                                self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
                                self.progress_lbl.setVisible(False)
                            QTimer.singleShot(3000, revert_default)
                        else:
                            self.gen_btn.setStyleSheet("")
                            self.gen_btn.setText("⚡ Generate Subtitles")
                            self.gen_btn.setEnabled(True)
                            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
                            self.progress_lbl.setVisible(False)
                    QTimer.singleShot(0, reset_btn)

        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def run_translation(self):
        srt_path = self.trans_srt_path.text().strip()
        target_lang = self.trans_lang.currentText()
        romanize = self.trans_romanize.isChecked()

        if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return self.show_error("SRT file not found!")

        self.trans_btn.setEnabled(False)
        def _task():
            try:
                self.signals.update_status.emit("Parsing SRT...")
                segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
                target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
                
                self.signals.update_status.emit(f"Translating {len(segments)} lines...")
                translate_batch(segments, target_code, romanize)
                
                srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                      for i, s in enumerate(segments))
                base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
                suffix = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
                save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}{suffix}.srt"))
                with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
                
                self.signals.update_status.emit("✅ Translation Done!")
                self.signals.populate_tabs.emit(save_path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
            finally:
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self.trans_btn.setEnabled(True))
        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def run_auto_sync(self):
        srt_path = self.sync_srt_path.text().strip()
        video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        if not srt_path or not video_path: return self.show_error("Video and SRT needed!")
        self.auto_sync_btn.setEnabled(False)
        def _task():
            try:
                offset, info = detect_sync_offset(video_path, srt_path, lambda s: self.signals.update_status.emit(s))
                if offset is None: raise Exception(info)
                self.signals.sync_offset_detected.emit(f"{offset:+.2f}", info)
            except Exception as e:
                self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
            finally:
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self.auto_sync_btn.setEnabled(True))
        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def on_sync_offset_detected(self, offset_str, info):
        self.sync_offset.setText(offset_str)
        self.set_status(f"✅ Auto-detected offset: {offset_str}s")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Auto-Sync", f"Detected Offset: {offset_str}s\n\n{info}\nClick 'Sync & Apply' to save!")

    def run_sync(self):
        srt_path = self.sync_srt_path.text().strip()
        try: offset_val = float(self.sync_offset.text().strip().replace('+', ''))
        except: return self.show_error("Offset must be a number")
        
        self.sync_btn.setEnabled(False)
        def _task():
            try:
                segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
                for seg in segments:
                    seg['start'] = max(0, seg['start'] + offset_val)
                    seg['end'] = max(0.001, seg['end'] + offset_val)
                srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                      for i, s in enumerate(segments))
                base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0].replace('_synced', '')
                save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_synced.srt"))
                with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
                self.signals.update_status.emit(f"✅ Sync done! Offset: {offset_val:+.2f}s")
                self.signals.populate_tabs.emit(save_path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
            finally:
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self.sync_btn.setEnabled(True))
        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def run_export_ass(self):
        srt_path = self.cust_srt_path.text().strip()
        if not srt_path: return self.show_error("No SRT selected")
        
        self.export_btn.setEnabled(False)
        def _task():
            try:
                segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
                
                # ASS Alignment Mapping (Numpad layout)
                pos_text = self.cust_position.currentText()
                alignment_map = {
                    "Bottom Left": 1, "Bottom Center": 2, "Bottom Right": 3,
                    "Middle Center": 5,
                    "Top Left": 7, "Top Center": 8, "Top Right": 9
                }
                alignment = alignment_map.get(pos_text, 2)
                
                style = {
                    "font_name": self.cust_font.currentText(),
                    "font_size": 20,
                    "primary_color": self.cust_color.text(),
                    "outline_color": "#000000",
                    "background": self.cust_bg.isChecked(),
                    "bold": False, "italic": False, "outline": 2, "shadow": 1, "alignment": alignment,
                }
                ass_content = generate_ass_content(segments, style)
                base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
                ass_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_styled.ass"))
                with open(ass_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(ass_content)
                self.signals.update_status.emit("✅ Exported .ASS")
                self.signals.populate_tabs.emit(ass_path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
            finally:
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self.export_btn.setEnabled(True))
        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def run_reframe(self):
        video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        if not video_path: return self.show_error("Select video in Generate tab!")
        self.ref_btn.setEnabled(False)
        def _task():
            try:
                out_path = reframe_video(video_path, self.ref_ratio.currentText(), "Blurred Background (Professional)")
                self.signals.update_status.emit("✅ Reframe done!")
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Saved to {out_path}"))
            except Exception as e:
                self.signals.error.emit(str(e))
            finally:
                QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: self.ref_btn.setEnabled(True))
        threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

    def run_export_srt_dialog(self):
        if not hasattr(self.timeline, 'segments') or not self.timeline.segments:
            return self.show_error("No subtitles loaded in timeline!")
            
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export SRT", "", "Subtitles (*.srt)")
        if save_path:
            try:
                from utils import format_timestamp
                srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                      for i, s in enumerate(self.timeline.segments))
                with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(srt_content)
                self.set_status(f"Exported SRT: {save_path}")
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", "SRT Exported Successfully!")
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_error(f"Failed to export SRT: {e}")

    def run_export_ass_dialog(self):
        if not hasattr(self.timeline, 'segments') or not self.timeline.segments:
            return self.show_error("No subtitles loaded in timeline!")
            
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export ASS", "", "Advanced Subtitles (*.ass)")
        if save_path:
            try:
                # Use current customize tab settings
                pos_text = self.cust_position.currentText()
                alignment_map = {
                    "Bottom Left": 1, "Bottom Center": 2, "Bottom Right": 3,
                    "Middle Center": 5, "Top Left": 7, "Top Center": 8, "Top Right": 9
                }
                style = {
                    "font_name": self.cust_font.currentText(),
                    "font_size": 20,
                    "primary_color": self.cust_color.text(),
                    "outline_color": "#000000",
                    "background": self.cust_bg.isChecked(),
                    "bold": False, "italic": False, "outline": 2, "shadow": 1, "alignment": alignment_map.get(pos_text, 2),
                }
                ass_content = generate_ass_content(self.timeline.segments, style)
                with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(ass_content)
                self.set_status(f"Exported ASS: {save_path}")
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", "ASS Exported Successfully!")
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_error(f"Failed to export ASS: {e}")

    def preview_external_vlc(self):
        video_path = self.gen_video_path.text().strip()
        if not video_path:
            return self.show_error("Please select a video file first!")
            
        # We need an SRT or ASS to preview. Let's try to export current customized ASS to a temp file, or use timeline srt
        if not hasattr(self.timeline, 'segments') or not self.timeline.segments:
            return self.show_error("No subtitles available to preview!")
            
        import tempfile
        # Create temp ASS
        pos_text = self.cust_position.currentText()
        alignment_map = {
            "Bottom Left": 1, "Bottom Center": 2, "Bottom Right": 3,
            "Middle Center": 5, "Top Left": 7, "Top Center": 8, "Top Right": 9
        }
        style = {
            "font_name": self.cust_font.currentText(),
            "font_size": 20,
            "primary_color": self.cust_color.text(),
            "outline_color": "#000000",
            "background": self.cust_bg.isChecked(),
            "bold": False, "italic": False, "outline": 2, "shadow": 1, "alignment": alignment_map.get(pos_text, 2),
        }
        ass_content = generate_ass_content(self.timeline.segments, style)
        
        temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
        temp_sub = os.path.join(temp_dir, "vsub_preview.ass")
        with open(temp_sub, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(ass_content)
            
        # Stop internal player so VLC can lock the file if needed
        self.player.stop()
        
        # Launch external VLC
        # Find vlc.exe in our local folder first, else try system PATH
        vlc_exe = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'vlc_64', 'vlc.exe')
        if not os.path.exists(vlc_exe):
            vlc_exe = "vlc"  # fallback to system PATH
            
        try:
            # We don't wait for it to finish
            subprocess.Popen([vlc_exe, video_path, f"--sub-file={temp_sub}"])
            self.set_status("Launched external VLC preview")
        except Exception as e:
            self.show_error(f"Failed to launch external VLC. Is it installed? {e}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    window = MainWindow()
    window.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec())


============================================================
FILE: .\reframing.py
============================================================

import os
import subprocess
from utils import clean_path, get_ffmpeg_path

def reframe_video(video_path, target_ratio="9:16", mode="Blurred Background (Professional)"):
    video_path = clean_path(video_path)
    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError("Video not found.")

    # Parse ratio (e.g., "9:16" -> w=9, h=16)
    try:
        r_w, r_h = target_ratio.split(':')
    except:
        r_w, r_h = "9", "16"

    # Calculate target dimensions that enclose the original video
    # ceil(.../2)*2 ensures the dimensions are even numbers (required by h264)
    tw = f"ceil(max(iw,ih*{r_w}/{r_h})/2)*2"
    th = f"ceil(max(ih,iw*{r_h}/{r_w})/2)*2"

    if mode == "Center Crop (Fill)":
        # Scale to fill the bounding box, then crop to exact box
        vf = f"scale={tw}:{th}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={tw}:{th}"
    else:
        # Blurred Background (Professional)
        vf = (
            f"[0:v]split=2[bg_in][fg_in];"
            f"[bg_in]scale={tw}:{th}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={tw}:{th},boxblur=20:20[bg];"
            f"[fg_in]scale={tw}:{th}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease[fg];"
            f"[bg][fg]overlay=(W-w)/2:(H-h)/2"
        )

    base, ext = os.path.splitext(video_path)
    safe_ratio = target_ratio.replace(':', 'x')
    safe_mode = mode.split(' ')[0].lower()
    out_path = f"{base}_reframed_{safe_ratio}_{safe_mode}{ext}"

    ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
    cmd = [
        ffmpeg_path, "-y", "-i", video_path,
        "-filter_complex" if "[0:v]" in vf else "-vf", vf,
        "-c:a", "copy", out_path
    ]

    cf = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, creationflags=cf)

    if proc.returncode != 0:
        err_msg = proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')
        raise Exception(f"FFmpeg reframing failed:\n{err_msg[-500:]}")

    return out_path


============================================================
FILE: .\security.py
============================================================

import keyring
import os
import base64

SERVICE_NAME = "UniversalSubtitleToolkit"
ACCOUNT_NAME = "api_key_secure_storage"

# Fallback path if Windows Vault is blocked
try:
    FALLBACK_FILE = os.path.join(os.getenv('APPDATA'), 'UniversalSubtitles', '.secure_key')
except Exception:
    FALLBACK_FILE = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), '.secure_key')

def save_api_key(api_key):
    """Saves the API key securely."""
    if api_key and api_key.strip():
        try:
            # Try Windows Credential Manager first
            keyring.set_password(SERVICE_NAME, ACCOUNT_NAME, api_key.strip())
        except Exception:
            # Fallback: Base64 obfuscated file in AppData
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(FALLBACK_FILE), exist_ok=True)
            with open(FALLBACK_FILE, "wb") as f:
                f.write(base64.b64encode(api_key.strip().encode()))

def load_api_key():
    """Retrieves the API key securely."""
    try:
        key = keyring.get_password(SERVICE_NAME, ACCOUNT_NAME)
        if key: return key
    except Exception:
        pass
    
    # Check fallback if Windows vault failed
    if os.path.exists(FALLBACK_FILE):
        try:
            with open(FALLBACK_FILE, "rb") as f:
                return base64.b64decode(f.read()).decode()
        except Exception:
            pass
            
    return ""
