============================================================
FILE: .\ui_timeline.py
============================================================

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QPen, QBrush, QFont, QFontMetrics, QPainterPath, QLinearGradient
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QRectF, QPoint, Signal

class TimelineWidget(QWidget):
    seek_requested = Signal(int)
    subtitle_dropped = Signal(str)
    subtitle_clicked = Signal()
    subtitle_edited = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setMinimumHeight(120)
        self.duration_ms = 0
        self.current_time_ms = 0
        self.segments = [] # List of dicts: {'start': ms, 'end': ms, 'text': str}
        
        # UI settings
        self.pixels_per_second = 50  # zoom level
        self.scroll_x = 0
        self.is_panning = False
        self.pan_last_x = 0

    def set_duration(self, duration_ms):
        self.duration_ms = max(duration_ms, 1)
        self.update()

    def set_current_time(self, time_ms):
        if time_ms == self.current_time_ms:
            return  # No change (e.g. paused), don't force auto-scroll
            
        is_playing = abs(time_ms - self.current_time_ms) < 1000
        self.current_time_ms = time_ms
        
        if not self.is_panning:
            playhead_x = (self.current_time_ms / 1000.0) * self.pixels_per_second
            
            if is_playing:
                # During playback, only scroll when playhead hits the right edge (90%)
                if playhead_x - self.scroll_x > self.width() * 0.9:
                    self.scroll_x = max(0, playhead_x - self.width() * 0.1)
            else:
                # If user jumped/seeked, center the playhead if it's off-screen
                if playhead_x < self.scroll_x or playhead_x > self.scroll_x + self.width():
                    self.scroll_x = max(0, playhead_x - self.width() / 2)
                
        self.update()

    def set_segments(self, segments):
        self.segments = segments
        self.update()

    def format_time(self, ms):
        s = int(ms / 1000)
        h = s // 3600
        m = (s % 3600) // 60
        s = s % 60
        if h > 0:
            return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
        return f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)

        rect = self.rect()
        width = rect.width()
        height = rect.height()

        # Background
        painter.fillRect(rect, QColor("#141414"))

        if self.duration_ms <= 0:
            # Draw empty state for video
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#444"), 2, Qt.DashLine))
            box_rect = rect.adjusted(10, 10, -10, -10)
            painter.drawRoundedRect(box_rect, 10, 10)
            painter.setPen(QColor("#666"))
            painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 12))
            painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignCenter, "Load a video to unlock timeline")
            return

        # Subtitle track area background
        sub_track = QRectF(0, 30, width, height - 30)
        painter.fillRect(sub_track, QColor("#1a1a1a"))

        if len(self.segments) == 0:
            # Empty state for subtitles
            is_hover = getattr(self, 'is_hovering_sub', False)
            color = "#ff9900" if is_hover else "#666"
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(color), 2, Qt.DashLine))
            box_rect = QRectF(10, 40, width - 20, height - 50)
            painter.drawRoundedRect(box_rect, 10, 10)
            painter.setPen(QColor(color))
            painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 12))
            painter.drawText(box_rect, Qt.AlignCenter, "📁 Click here or Drag & Drop a .srt file to add subtitles")

        # Time track area (top 30px)
        track_rect = QRectF(0, 0, width, 30)
        painter.fillRect(track_rect, QColor("#1e1e1e"))

        # Draw ticks and time labels
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#666666")))
        font = QFont("Segoe UI", 8)
        painter.setFont(font)
        
        visible_start_s = self.scroll_x / self.pixels_per_second
        visible_end_s = (self.scroll_x + width) / self.pixels_per_second
        
        # Tick interval logic
        tick_interval_s = 10 # 10 seconds default
        if self.pixels_per_second < 10:
            tick_interval_s = 60
        elif self.pixels_per_second > 100:
            tick_interval_s = 5

        start_tick = int(visible_start_s // tick_interval_s) * tick_interval_s
        
        for t in range(start_tick, int(visible_end_s) + tick_interval_s, tick_interval_s):
            x = (t * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
            if x < 0 or x > width:
                continue
                
            # Major tick
            painter.drawLine(x, 15, x, 30)
            
            # Minor ticks (every 1 second if zoomed in enough)
            if self.pixels_per_second >= 20:
                minor_interval = tick_interval_s // 5
                for mt in range(1, 5):
                    mx = ((t + mt * minor_interval) * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
                    if 0 <= mx <= width:
                        painter.drawLine(mx, 22, mx, 30)
                        
            time_str = self.format_time(t * 1000)
            painter.setPen(QColor("#aaaaaa"))
            painter.drawText(QRectF(x - 25, 0, 50, 15), Qt.AlignCenter, time_str)
            painter.setPen(QColor("#666666"))

        # Draw segments
        font.setPointSize(9)
        painter.setFont(font)
        fm = QFontMetrics(font)
        
        for seg in self.segments:
            start_s = seg['start']
            end_s = seg['end']
            
            x_start = (start_s * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
            x_end = (end_s * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
            
            if x_end < 0 or x_start > width:
                continue # Outside visible area
                
            seg_width = max(2, x_end - x_start)
            seg_rect = QRectF(x_start, 40, seg_width, height - 60)
            
            # Subtitle Block Background (translucent dark)
            painter.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor(30, 30, 30, 200)))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(seg_rect, 6, 6)
            
            # Subtitle Block Border (Orange)
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#ff9900"), 1))
            painter.setBrush(Qt.NoBrush)
            painter.drawRoundedRect(seg_rect, 6, 6)
            
            # Top little handles/decorations
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor("#ff9900")))
            painter.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
            painter.drawPolygon([
                QPoint(int(x_start + seg_width/2 - 4), 36),
                QPoint(int(x_start + seg_width/2 + 4), 36),
                QPoint(int(x_start + seg_width/2), 40)
            ])

            # Text inside
            painter.setPen(QPen(Qt.white))
            text_rect = seg_rect.adjusted(6, 4, -6, -4)
            # Elide text if it's too long
            elided_text = fm.elidedText(seg['text'].replace('\n', ' '), Qt.ElideRight, int(text_rect.width()))
            painter.drawText(text_rect, Qt.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignTop | Qt.TextWordWrap, elided_text)

        # Draw playhead
        playhead_x = (self.current_time_ms / 1000.0 * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
        if 0 <= playhead_x <= width:
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#ff9900"), 2))
            painter.drawLine(playhead_x, 0, playhead_x, height)
            
            # Playhead top handle
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor("#ff9900")))
            painter.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
            painter.drawRect(playhead_x - 4, 0, 8, 10)
            painter.drawPolygon([
                QPoint(int(playhead_x - 4), 10),
                QPoint(int(playhead_x + 4), 10),
                QPoint(int(playhead_x), 15)
            ])

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            x = event.position().x()
            y = event.position().y()
            
            # If we have a duration but no subtitles, clicking the bottom area opens file dialog
            if self.duration_ms > 0 and len(self.segments) == 0 and y > 30:
                self.subtitle_clicked.emit()
                return
                
            # If clicked in the top ruler area (y <= 30) or near the playhead, we scrub
            playhead_x = (self.current_time_ms / 1000.0 * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
            is_near_playhead = abs(x - playhead_x) < 15
            
            if y <= 30 or is_near_playhead:
                self.is_scrubbing = True
                self.handle_scrub(x)
            else:
                self.is_panning = True
                self.pan_last_x = x
                self.setCursor(Qt.ClosedHandCursor)

    def handle_scrub(self, mouse_x):
        time_s = (mouse_x + self.scroll_x) / self.pixels_per_second
        time_ms = int(time_s * 1000)
        time_ms = max(0, min(time_ms, self.duration_ms))
        self.seek_requested.emit(time_ms)
        self.set_current_time(time_ms)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.is_panning = False
            if hasattr(self, 'is_scrubbing'):
                self.is_scrubbing = False
            self.setCursor(Qt.ArrowCursor)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if hasattr(self, 'is_scrubbing') and self.is_scrubbing:
            self.handle_scrub(event.position().x())
        elif self.is_panning:
            dx = event.position().x() - self.pan_last_x
            self.scroll_x -= dx
            self.scroll_x = max(0, self.scroll_x)
            self.pan_last_x = event.position().x()
            self.update()

    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls() and self.duration_ms > 0:
            event.acceptProposedAction()
            self.is_hovering_sub = True
            self.update()
            
    def dragLeaveEvent(self, event):
        self.is_hovering_sub = False
        self.update()
        
    def dropEvent(self, event):
        self.is_hovering_sub = False
        self.update()
        urls = event.mimeData().urls()
        if urls:
            path = urls[0].toLocalFile()
            if path.lower().endswith(('.srt', '.ass')):
                self.subtitle_dropped.emit(path)

    def wheelEvent(self, event):
        delta_y = event.angleDelta().y()
        delta_x = event.angleDelta().x()

        if delta_y != 0:
            # Zoom in/out with scroll wheel (vertical)
            zoom_factor = 1.1 if delta_y > 0 else 0.9
            old_pixels_per_second = self.pixels_per_second
            self.pixels_per_second *= zoom_factor
            
            # Clamp zoom
            self.pixels_per_second = max(5, min(500, self.pixels_per_second))
            
            # Adjust scroll_x so the time under cursor stays in place
            mouse_x = event.position().x()
            time_under_cursor = (self.scroll_x + mouse_x) / old_pixels_per_second
            self.scroll_x = (time_under_cursor * self.pixels_per_second) - mouse_x
            self.scroll_x = max(0, self.scroll_x)
            
        if delta_x != 0:
            # Pan timeline with horizontal slide (trackpad)
            # Some trackpads send negative delta for right slide, some positive.
            self.scroll_x -= delta_x
            self.scroll_x = max(0, self.scroll_x)
            
        self.update()

    def mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            x = event.position().x()
            y = event.position().y()
            
            # Check if clicked inside any segment
            for i, seg in enumerate(self.segments):
                start_s = seg['start']
                end_s = seg['end']
                
                x_start = (start_s * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
                x_end = (end_s * self.pixels_per_second) - self.scroll_x
                seg_width = max(2, x_end - x_start)
                
                # Check click bounds
                if x_start <= x <= x_start + seg_width and 40 <= y <= self.height() - 20:
                    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QInputDialog
                    new_text, ok = QInputDialog.getMultiLineText(self, "Edit Subtitle", "Subtitle Text:", seg['text'])
                    if ok and new_text:
                        self.segments[i]['text'] = new_text.strip()
                        self.update()
                        
                        # Save back to file if we know the path
                        if hasattr(self, 'srt_path') and self.srt_path:
                            self.save_srt()
                            self.subtitle_edited.emit(self.srt_path)
                    return

    def save_srt(self):
        try:
            from utils import format_timestamp
            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(self.segments))
            with open(self.srt_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(srt_content)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Failed to save edited subtitle:", e)

FILE: .\ui_video_player.py

import os
import sys

# Ensure VLC DLLs can be found before importing anything that imports vlc
vlc_dir = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'vlc_64')
if os.path.exists(vlc_dir):
    os.environ['PYTHON_VLC_MODULE_PATH'] = vlc_dir
    if hasattr(os, 'add_dll_directory'):
        os.add_dll_directory(vlc_dir)

import vlc
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal

class DropOverlay(QLabel):
    file_dropped = Signal(str)
    clicked = Signal()
    
    def __init__(self, text, parent=None):
        super().__init__(text, parent)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.reset_style()
        
    def reset_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                border: 3px dashed #666;
                border-radius: 15px;
                color: #888;
                font-size: 18px;
                font-weight: bold;
                background-color: rgba(20, 20, 20, 200);
            }
            QLabel:hover {
                border-color: #ff9900;
                color: #ff9900;
                background-color: rgba(40, 40, 40, 220);
            }
        """)

    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            event.acceptProposedAction()
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel {
                    border: 3px dashed #ff9900;
                    border-radius: 15px;
                    color: #ff9900;
                    font-size: 18px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                    background-color: rgba(40, 40, 40, 220);
                }
            """)
            
    def dragLeaveEvent(self, event):
        self.reset_style()
            
    def dropEvent(self, event):
        self.reset_style()
        urls = event.mimeData().urls()
        if urls:
            path = urls[0].toLocalFile()
            if path.lower().endswith(('.mp4', '.mkv', '.avi', '.ts', '.mov')):
                self.file_dropped.emit(path)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.clicked.emit()

class VLCPlayerWidget(QWidget):
    file_dropped = Signal(str)
    overlay_clicked = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        self.video_frame = QWidget(self)
        self.video_frame.setStyleSheet("background-color: black;")
        
        vf_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.video_frame)
        vf_layout.setContentsMargins(50, 50, 50, 50)
        
        self.overlay = DropOverlay("📁 Click to add video or drag video here to load", self.video_frame)
        self.overlay.file_dropped.connect(self.file_dropped.emit)
        self.overlay.clicked.connect(self.overlay_clicked.emit)
        vf_layout.addWidget(self.overlay)
        
        self.layout.addWidget(self.video_frame)
        
        # Determine VLC paths based on vlc_64 in current dir or system
        vlc_plugin_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'vlc_64', 'plugins')
        if os.path.exists(vlc_plugin_path):
            os.environ['VLC_PLUGIN_PATH'] = vlc_plugin_path
            
        args = [
            "--no-xlib",
            "--quiet",
            "--ignore-config",
        ]
        
        self.instance = vlc.Instance(args)
        self.media_player = self.instance.media_player_new()
        
        # Platform specific window handle binding
        if sys.platform.startswith('linux'): # for Linux using the X Server
            self.media_player.set_xwindow(self.video_frame.winId())
        elif sys.platform == "win32": # for Windows
            self.media_player.set_hwnd(self.video_frame.winId())
        elif sys.platform == "darwin": # for MacOS
            try:
                self.media_player.set_nsobject(int(self.video_frame.winId()))
            except:
                self.media_player.set_nsobject(self.video_frame.winId())

    def play(self, video_path):
        self.overlay.hide()
        media = self.instance.media_new(video_path)
        self.media_player.set_media(media)
        self.media_player.play()

    def set_subtitle(self, srt_path, force=False):
        if srt_path and os.path.exists(srt_path):
            file_url = "file:///" + srt_path.replace('\\', '/')
            self.media_player.video_set_spu(-1)  # Disable embedded subtitles
            self.media_player.add_slave(vlc.MediaSlaveType.subtitle, file_url, True)

    def pause(self):
        self.media_player.pause()

    def stop(self):
        self.media_player.stop()

    def get_time(self):
        return self.media_player.get_time() # in ms

    def set_time(self, time_ms):
        self.media_player.set_time(time_ms)
        
    def get_length(self):
        return self.media_player.get_length() # in ms


============================================================
FILE: .\ui_windows.py

import tkinter as tk
import tkinter.filedialog as filedialog
import tkinter.messagebox as messagebox
import subprocess
import os
import shutil
import ust_settings as config
from utils import clean_path, parse_srt, format_timestamp

def show_srt_preview(app, srt_path, title="👁 Subtitle Preview"):
    if not srt_path:
        return messagebox.showerror("Preview", "No subtitle file selected!")
    if not os.path.exists(srt_path):
        return messagebox.showerror("Preview", f"File not found:\n{srt_path}")
    try:
        segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
    except Exception as e:
        return messagebox.showerror("Preview", f"Could not read file:\n{e}")

    pw = tk.Toplevel(app)
    pw.title(title)
    pw.geometry("620x520")
    pw.configure(bg=config.DARK_BG)
    pw.transient(app)
    pw.resizable(True, True)

    hf = tk.Frame(pw, bg=config.DARK_BG); hf.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=15, pady=(10, 4))
    tk.Label(hf, text=os.path.basename(srt_path), bg=config.DARK_BG, fg=config.VLC_ORANGE,
             font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold")).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    tk.Label(hf, text=f"  {len(segments)} lines", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg="#888",
             font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(side=tk.LEFT)

    sf = tk.Frame(pw, bg=config.DARK_BG); sf.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=15, pady=(0, 4))
    search_var = tk.StringVar()
    tk.Label(sf, text="Search:", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg="#888", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    search_entry = tk.Entry(sf, textvariable=search_var, width=28, bg="#2a2a2a", fg="white",
                            insertbackground="white", border=0, font=("Segoe UI", 9))
    search_entry.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5, ipady=2)
    match_label = tk.Label(sf, text="", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg=config.VLC_ORANGE, font=("Segoe UI", 8))
    match_label.pack(side=tk.LEFT)

    tf = tk.Frame(pw, bg=config.DARK_BG); tf.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=15, pady=4)
    vsb = tk.Scrollbar(tf); vsb.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, fill=tk.Y)
    txt = tk.Text(tf, bg="#0d0d0d", fg="white", font=("Consolas", 9), yscrollcommand=vsb.set,
                  border=0, highlightthickness=0, wrap=tk.WORD, selectbackground=config.VLC_ORANGE)
    txt.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
    vsb.config(command=txt.yview)

    txt.tag_configure("idx",   foreground="#555",     font=("Consolas", 8))
    txt.tag_configure("time",  foreground=config.VLC_ORANGE, font=("Consolas", 9, "bold"))
    txt.tag_configure("body",  foreground="#e8e8e8",  font=("Consolas", 9))
    txt.tag_configure("sep",   foreground="#252525")
    txt.tag_configure("found", background=config.VLC_ORANGE, foreground="black")

    for seg in segments:
        txt.insert(tk.END, f"[{seg['index']}]  ", "idx")
        txt.insert(tk.END, f"{format_timestamp(seg['start'])}  →  {format_timestamp(seg['end'])}\n", "time")
        txt.insert(tk.END, f"{seg['text']}\n", "body")
        txt.insert(tk.END, "─" * 70 + "\n", "sep")
    txt.config(state=tk.DISABLED)

    def do_search(*_):
        txt.tag_remove("found", "1.0", tk.END)
        q = search_var.get().strip()
        if not q:
            match_label.config(text=""); return
        count, start = 0, "1.0"
        while True:
            pos = txt.search(q, start, nocase=True, stopindex=tk.END)
            if not pos: break
            end = f"{pos}+{len(q)}c"
            txt.tag_add("found", pos, end)
            if count == 0: txt.see(pos)
            count += 1
            start = end
        match_label.config(text=f"{count} match{'es' if count != 1 else ''}")
    search_var.trace_add("write", do_search)
    search_entry.bind("<Return>", do_search)

    bf = tk.Frame(pw, bg=config.DARK_BG); bf.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=15, pady=(4, 10))
    tk.Button(bf, text="📋 Copy All", bg="#333", fg="white", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9), padx=8, pady=4,
              command=lambda: (pw.clipboard_clear(), pw.clipboard_append(open(srt_path, encoding='utf-8').read()))
             ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 5))
    tk.Button(bf, text="📝 Open in Notepad", bg="#333", fg="white", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9), padx=8, pady=4,
              command=lambda: subprocess.Popen(["notepad", srt_path])).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    tk.Button(bf, text="Close", bg=config.VLC_ORANGE, fg="black", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"),
              padx=12, pady=4, command=pw.destroy).pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

def show_style_preview(app, style_vars):
    pw = tk.Toplevel(app)
    pw.title("🖥 Live Style Preview")
    pw.geometry("660x420")
    pw.configure(bg=config.DARK_BG)
    pw.transient(app)
    pw.resizable(False, False)

    tk.Label(pw, text="Live Style Preview", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg=config.VLC_ORANGE,
             font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold")).pack(pady=(10, 4))
    tk.Label(pw, text="Preview updates automatically as you change settings",
             bg=config.DARK_BG, fg="#555", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack(pady=(0, 6))

    CWIDTH, CHEIGHT = 620, 280
    canvas = tk.Canvas(pw, width=CWIDTH, height=CHEIGHT, bg="#000",
                       highlightthickness=2, highlightbackground="#333")
    canvas.pack(padx=20)

    def get_initial_text():
        sp = clean_path(style_vars['srt_path'].get().strip())
        if sp and os.path.exists(sp):
            segs = parse_srt(sp)
            if segs: return segs[0]['text'].replace('\n', ' ')[:60]
        return "Sample subtitle text – preview here"

    def draw():
        canvas.delete("all")
        canvas.create_rectangle(0, 0, CWIDTH, CHEIGHT, fill="#444444", outline="")
        for x in range(0, CWIDTH, 20):
            for y in range(0, CHEIGHT, 20):
                if (x // 20 + y // 20) % 2 == 0:
                    canvas.create_rectangle(x, y, x+20, y+20, fill="#3c3c3c", outline="")

        canvas.create_rectangle(0, 0, CWIDTH, 25, fill="#111111", outline="")
        canvas.create_rectangle(0, CHEIGHT-25, CWIDTH, CHEIGHT, fill="#111111", outline="")

        font_name   = style_vars['font_name'].get() or "Arial"
        font_size   = style_vars['font_size'].get()
        weight      = " ".join(filter(None, ["bold" if style_vars['bold'].get() else "", "italic" if style_vars['italic'].get() else ""])) or "normal"
        try:
            fnt = (font_name, -font_size, weight)
            tmp = canvas.create_text(CWIDTH//2, 10, text="A", fill="#000000", font=fnt)
            canvas.delete(tmp)
        except Exception:
            fnt = ("Arial", -font_size, weight)

        text_col    = style_vars['text_color'].get()
        outline_col = style_vars['outline_color'].get()
        outline_sz  = style_vars['outline_sz'].get()
        shadow_sz   = style_vars['shadow'].get()
        pos         = style_vars['position'].get()
        display_txt = entry_widget.get().strip() or " "

        cx, cy = CWIDTH // 2, 38 if pos == "top" else CHEIGHT - 38

        if style_vars['bg'].get():
            tmp = canvas.create_text(cx, cy, text=display_txt, font=fnt, anchor="center")
            bbox = canvas.bbox(tmp)
            canvas.delete(tmp)
            if bbox:
                x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
                canvas.create_rectangle(x1 - 10, y1 - 2, x2 + 10, y2 + 2, fill="#111111", outline="")

        if shadow_sz > 0:
            for d in range(shadow_sz, 0, -1):
                alpha = "#1a1a1a" if d == shadow_sz else "#0d0d0d"
                canvas.create_text(cx + d*2, cy + d*2, text=display_txt, fill=alpha, font=fnt, anchor="center")

        if outline_sz > 0:
            for dx in range(-outline_sz, outline_sz + 1):
                for dy in range(-outline_sz, outline_sz + 1):
                    if abs(dx) + abs(dy) >= outline_sz:
                        canvas.create_text(cx + dx, cy + dy, text=display_txt, fill=outline_col, font=fnt, anchor="center")

        canvas.create_text(cx, cy, text=display_txt, fill=text_col, font=fnt, anchor="center")
        indicator = "▲ Top" if pos == "top" else "▼ Bottom"
        canvas.create_text(CWIDTH - 8, CHEIGHT - 8, text=indicator, fill="#333", font=("Segoe UI", 7), anchor="se")

    ctrl = tk.Frame(pw, bg=config.DARK_BG); ctrl.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=20, pady=8)
    tk.Label(ctrl, text="Preview text:", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg="#888", font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    entry_widget = tk.Entry(ctrl, width=32, bg="#2a2a2a", fg="white", insertbackground="white", border=0, font=("Segoe UI", 9))
    entry_widget.insert(0, get_initial_text())
    entry_widget.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=6, ipady=2)
    tk.Button(ctrl, text="Use 1st subtitle line", bg="#252525", fg="#aaa", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 8), padx=6, pady=3,
              command=lambda: [entry_widget.delete(0, tk.END), entry_widget.insert(0, get_initial_text()), draw()]
             ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=4)

    def live_update(*args):
        if canvas.winfo_exists(): draw()

    entry_widget.bind("<KeyRelease>", live_update)
    pw.live_update_ref = live_update 
    
    traces = []
    def add_trace(var):
        cb = var.trace_add("write", live_update)
        traces.append((var, cb))

    for key, var in style_vars.items():
        if key != 'srt_path': add_trace(var)

    def on_close():
        for key, var in style_vars.items():
            if key != 'srt_path':
                try: var.trace_remove("write", traces.pop(0)[1])
                except: pass
        pw.destroy()

    pw.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", on_close)
    draw()

def open_history(app, populate_tabs_cb):
    hw = tk.Toplevel(app)
    hw.title("Subtitle History")
    hw.geometry("540x400")
    hw.configure(bg=config.DARK_BG)
    hw.transient(app)

    tk.Label(hw, text="Generated Subtitles History", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg=config.VLC_ORANGE,
             font=("Segoe UI", 12, "bold")).pack(pady=10)

    lf = tk.Frame(hw, bg=config.DARK_BG); lf.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=15, pady=5)
    sb = tk.Scrollbar(lf); sb.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, fill=tk.Y)
    lb = tk.Listbox(lf, bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="white", yscrollcommand=sb.set, selectbackground=config.VLC_ORANGE,
                    selectforeground="black", border=0, highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground="#333", font=("Segoe UI", 9))
    lb.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
    sb.config(command=lb.yview)

    def refresh():
        lb.delete(0, tk.END)
        if os.path.exists(config.SUB_DIR):
            files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(config.SUB_DIR) if f.lower().endswith(('.srt', '.ass'))],
                           key=lambda x: os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, x)), reverse=True)
            for f in files: lb.insert(tk.END, f)
    refresh()

    def load_sel():
        sel = lb.curselection()
        if not sel: return
        populate_tabs_cb(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, lb.get(sel[0])))
        messagebox.showinfo("Loaded", "Subtitle loaded into all tabs!", parent=hw)

    def del_sel():
        sel = lb.curselection()
        if not sel: return
        fn = lb.get(sel[0])
        if messagebox.askyesno("Confirm", f"Delete '{fn}'?", parent=hw):
            os.remove(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, fn)); refresh()

    def export_sel():
        sel = lb.curselection()
        if not sel: return
        fn  = lb.get(sel[0])
        ext = os.path.splitext(fn)[1]
        dst = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(defaultextension=ext, initialfile=fn, title="Save As…", filetypes=[("Subtitle", f"*{ext}")], parent=hw)
        if dst:
            shutil.copy(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, fn), dst)
            messagebox.showinfo("Success", "Exported successfully!", parent=hw)

    bf = tk.Frame(hw, bg=config.DARK_BG); bf.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=10, padx=15)
    for txt, cmd, col in [
        ("📂 Load into Tabs", load_sel,  "#2196F3"),
        ("💾 Save As…",       export_sel, "#4CAF50"),
        ("🗑️ Delete",          del_sel,   "#cc0000"),
        ("📁 Open Folder",    lambda: os.startfile(config.SUB_DIR), "#444"),
    ]:
        tk.Button(bf, text=txt, bg=col, fg="white", font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), relief=tk.FLAT, command=cmd, padx=6, pady=5).pack(side=tk.LEFT, expand=True, fill=tk.X, padx=3)

from PyInstaller.utils.hooks import collect_all

datas = [('logo.ico', '.'), ('universal_subtitles.lua', '.'), ('vlc_64', 'vlc_64')]
binaries = [('ffmpeg.exe', 'bin')]
hiddenimports = []

# Collect online dependencies for the "Fast Online Download" feature
tmp_ret = collect_all('babelfish')
datas += tmp_ret[0]; binaries += tmp_ret[1]; hiddenimports += tmp_ret[2]
tmp_ret = collect_all('subliminal')
datas += tmp_ret[0]; binaries += tmp_ret[1]; hiddenimports += tmp_ret[2]
tmp_ret = collect_all('guessit')
datas += tmp_ret[0]; binaries += tmp_ret[1]; hiddenimports += tmp_ret[2]
tmp_ret = collect_all('customtkinter')
datas += tmp_ret[0]; binaries += tmp_ret[1]; hiddenimports += tmp_ret[2]

a = Analysis(
    ['main.py'],
    pathex=[],
    binaries=binaries,
    datas=datas,
    hiddenimports=hiddenimports,
    hookspath=[],
    hooksconfig={},
    runtime_hooks=[],
    excludes=['torch', 'torchaudio', 'faster_whisper', 'ctranslate2', 'onnxruntime', 'tokenizers'],
    noarchive=False,
    optimize=0,
)
pyz = PYZ(a.pure)

exe = EXE(
    pyz,
    a.scripts,
    [],
    exclude_binaries=True,
    name='UniversalSubtitles',
    debug=False,
    bootloader_ignore_signals=False,
    strip=False,
    upx=True,
    console=False,
    disable_windowed_traceback=False,
    argv_emulation=False,
    target_arch=None,
    codesign_identity=None,
    entitlements_file=None,
    icon=['logo.ico'],
)
coll = COLLECT(
    exe,
    a.binaries,
    a.datas,
    strip=False,
    upx=True,
    upx_exclude=[],
    name='UniversalSubtitles',
)

============================================================

import math
import os
import sys
import re
import ust_settings as config

def format_timestamp(seconds):
    seconds = max(0, seconds)
    h  = math.floor(seconds / 3600)
    m  = math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)
    s  = math.floor(seconds % 60)
    ms = math.floor((seconds - math.floor(seconds)) * 1000)
    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d},{ms:03d}"

def format_ass_timestamp(seconds):
    seconds = max(0, seconds)
    h  = int(seconds // 3600)
    m  = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)
    s  = int(seconds % 60)
    cs = int(round((seconds - int(seconds)) * 100))
    if cs >= 100: cs = 99
    return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}.{cs:02d}"

def time_to_sec(t_str):
    t_str = t_str.replace('.', ',')
    if ',' not in t_str: t_str += ',000'
    h, m, s_ms = t_str.split(':')
    s, ms = s_ms.split(',')
    return int(h)*3600 + int(m)*60 + int(s) + int(ms)/1000.0

def clean_path(path):
    if not path: return ""
    return os.path.normpath(path.replace("\\\\", "\\").replace("//", "/"))

def detect_paired_file(filepath, file_type):
    if not filepath: return ""
    base = os.path.splitext(filepath)[0]
    if file_type == "video":
        for ext in ['.mp4', '.mkv', '.avi', '.ts', '.mov', '.wmv']:
            if os.path.exists(base + ext): return base + ext
    elif file_type == "srt":
        if os.path.exists(base + ".srt"): return base + ".srt"
    return ""

def get_available_subtitles(video_path):
    subs = []
    if video_path and os.path.exists(video_path):
        v_dir = os.path.dirname(video_path)
        if os.path.exists(v_dir):
            for f in os.listdir(v_dir):
                if f.lower().endswith(('.srt', '.ass')):
                    subs.append(os.path.normpath(os.path.join(v_dir, f)))
    if os.path.exists(config.SUB_DIR):
        for f in os.listdir(config.SUB_DIR):
            if f.lower().endswith(('.srt', '.ass')):
                subs.append(os.path.normpath(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f)))
    return list(dict.fromkeys(subs))

def resource_path(relative_path):
    try:
        base_path = sys._MEIPASS
    except Exception:
        base_path = os.path.abspath(".")
    return os.path.join(base_path, relative_path)

def parse_srt(filepath):
    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
        content = f.read()
    blocks  = re.split(r'\n\s*\n', content.strip())
    parsed  = []
    for block in blocks:
        lines = block.split('\n')
        if len(lines) >= 3:
            times = lines[1].split(' --> ')
            if len(times) == 2:
                try:
                    parsed.append({
                        "index": lines[0].strip(),
                        "start": time_to_sec(times[0].strip()),
                        "end":   time_to_sec(times[1].strip()),
                        "text":  '\n'.join(lines[2:]).strip()
                    })
                except Exception:
                    pass
    return parsed

def hex_to_ass_color(hex_color, alpha=0):
    hx = hex_color.lstrip('#')
    if len(hx) == 3: hx = ''.join(c*2 for c in hx)
    r, g, b = int(hx[0:2], 16), int(hx[2:4], 16), int(hx[4:6], 16)
    return f"&H{alpha:02X}{b:02X}{g:02X}{r:02X}"

def generate_ass_content(segments, style):
    primary   = hex_to_ass_color(style['primary_color'])
    outline_c = hex_to_ass_color(style['outline_color'])
    bg_alpha  = 0x60 if style['background'] else 0xFF
    back      = hex_to_ass_color("#000000", alpha=bg_alpha)
    bold      = -1 if style['bold']   else 0
    italic    = -1 if style['italic'] else 0

    header = (
        "[Script Info]\n"
        "; Generated by Universal Subtitle Toolkit (VLC Edition)\n"
        "ScriptType: v4.00+\n"
        "WrapStyle: 0\n"
        "ScaledBorderAndShadow: yes\n"
        "PlayResX: 512\n"
        "PlayResY: 288\n\n"
        "[V4+ Styles]\n"
        "Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, "
        "OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, "
        "ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, "
        "Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\n"
        f"Style: Default,{style['font_name']},{style['font_size']},"
        f"{primary},&H000000FF,{outline_c},{back},"
        f"{bold},{italic},0,0,100,100,0,0,1,"
        f"{style['outline']},{style['shadow']},{style['alignment']},10,10,10,1\n\n"
        "[Events]\n"
        "Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\n"
    )

    events = ""
    for seg in segments:
        text = seg['text'].replace('\n', '\\N').strip()
        if text:
            events += (
                f"Dialogue: 0,"
                f"{format_ass_timestamp(seg['start'])},"
                f"{format_ass_timestamp(seg['end'])},"
                f"Default,,0,0,0,,{text}\n"
            )
    return header + events

def get_ffmpeg_path():
    """Foolproof method to find ffmpeg.exe whether running as script or compiled .exe"""
    import sys, os
    
    paths_to_check = []
    
    # 1. Using sys.executable (Bundle Root)
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        exe_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
        paths_to_check.extend([
            os.path.join(exe_dir, "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
            os.path.join(exe_dir, "ffmpeg.exe"),
            os.path.join(exe_dir, "_internal", "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
            os.path.join(exe_dir, "_internal", "ffmpeg.exe"),
        ])
        
        # If executed via App Alias, sys.executable might be in LocalAppData\Microsoft\WindowsApps
        # We can use __file__ to get the real path to utils.pyc
        try:
            real_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
            paths_to_check.extend([
                os.path.join(real_dir, "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
                os.path.join(real_dir, "ffmpeg.exe"),
                os.path.join(real_dir, "..", "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
                os.path.join(real_dir, "..", "ffmpeg.exe"),
            ])
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 2. Using sys._MEIPASS (For PyInstaller onefile or 6.0+ onedir)
    try:
        meipass = sys._MEIPASS
        paths_to_check.extend([
            os.path.join(meipass, "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
            os.path.join(meipass, "ffmpeg.exe"),
        ])
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 3. Current working directory (Dev mode)
    paths_to_check.extend([
        os.path.join(os.path.abspath("."), "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"),
        os.path.join(os.path.abspath("."), "ffmpeg.exe"),
    ])

    # Find first valid path
    for p in paths_to_check:
        if os.path.exists(p):
            return p
            
    # Fallback to system PATH
    return "ffmpeg"

============================================================
FILE: .\vlc_integration.py

import os
import subprocess
import shutil
import tkinter.filedialog as filedialog
import tkinter.messagebox as messagebox
from utils import clean_path, resource_path

def get_vlc_path():
    paths = [
        os.path.join(os.environ.get('PROGRAMW6432', 'C:\\Program Files'), 'VideoLAN', 'VLC', 'vlc.exe'),
        os.path.join(os.environ.get('PROGRAMFILES(X86)', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)'), 'VideoLAN', 'VLC', 'vlc.exe'),
        os.path.join(os.environ.get('PROGRAMFILES', 'C:\\Program Files'), 'VideoLAN', 'VLC', 'vlc.exe')
    ]
    for p in paths:
        if os.path.exists(p): return p
    return None

def launch_vlc_preview(video_path, srt_path):
    if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
        messagebox.showerror("VLC Preview", "No valid video file selected.\nPlease select a video in Tab 1 first!")
        return
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path):
        messagebox.showerror("VLC Preview", f"No valid subtitle file found at:\n{srt_path}")
        return
        
    vlc = get_vlc_path()
    if not vlc:
        messagebox.showerror("VLC Not Found", "Could not locate vlc.exe.\nPlease ensure VLC is installed in the default directory.")
        return
        
    try:
        cf = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
        
        vid = clean_path(video_path)
        sub = clean_path(srt_path)
        
        # Using a raw string guarantees VLC parses the quotes properly on Windows!
        cmd = f'"{vlc}" --one-instance --no-playlist-enqueue "{vid}" --sub-file="{sub}"'
        
        subprocess.Popen(
            cmd,
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            creationflags=cf
        )
    except Exception as e:
        messagebox.showerror("Error", f"Could not launch VLC:\n{e}")

def apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, subtitle_path, success_message, video_path=None):
    def _execute():
        import shutil
        import os
        from utils import clean_path
        import tkinter.messagebox as messagebox

        subtitle_to_signal = subtitle_path
        saved_next_to_video = False
        
        # 1. Attempt to save a copy NEXT TO THE VIDEO for auto-loading in the future
        if video_path and os.path.exists(video_path):
            try:
                video_dir = os.path.dirname(video_path)
                sub_name = os.path.basename(subtitle_path)
                target_sub_path = os.path.join(video_dir, sub_name)
                
                # Copy the file to the video's directory if it's not already there
                if os.path.abspath(subtitle_path) != os.path.abspath(target_sub_path):
                    shutil.copy2(subtitle_path, target_sub_path)
                
                subtitle_to_signal = target_sub_path
                saved_next_to_video = True
            except Exception:
                # If the video folder is read-only (like a DVD), skip copying
                pass

        # 2. Write to signal file for VLC Lua Extension to hot-load seamlessly
        try:
            appdata = os.environ.get('APPDATA', '')
            if appdata:
                signal_dir = os.path.join(appdata, 'UniversalSubtitles')
                os.makedirs(signal_dir, exist_ok=True)
                signal_file = os.path.join(signal_dir, 'apply_sub.txt')
                
                with open(signal_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(clean_path(subtitle_to_signal))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 3. Show success prompt
        msg = f"{success_message}\n\n"
        msg += "▶️ Subtitle sent to VLC Player seamlessly!\n"
        if saved_next_to_video:
            msg += "💾 A copy was saved next to your video for future auto-loading.\n\n"
        else:
            msg += "\n"
        msg += "Tip: If it doesn't appear automatically, click 'Apply Latest Subtitle' in your VLC Extension."
        
        app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showinfo("✅ Success", msg))

    app.after(0, _execute)

def get_vlc_ext_path():
    """Helper to get the exact path where the Lua extension should live."""
    return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Roaming', 'vlc', 'lua', 'extensions', 'universal_subtitles.lua')

def install_vlc_extension(app):
    lua_source = resource_path("universal_subtitles.lua")
    if not os.path.exists(lua_source):
        return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Could not find universal_subtitles.lua inside the application files.")

    ext_file = get_vlc_ext_path()
    target_dir = os.path.dirname(ext_file)

    try:
        os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    msg = "This will install the auto-load extension directly into VLC's plugin folder.\n\nClick OK to install it automatically."
    if messagebox.askokcancel("Install VLC Extension", msg, parent=app):
        try:
            shutil.copy2(lua_source, ext_file)
            messagebox.showinfo("✅ Installation Complete", f"Extension installed successfully!\n\nPlease completely close and restart VLC Player.", parent=app)
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror("Installation Failed", f"Could not copy file:\n{str(e)}", parent=app)

def remove_vlc_extension(app):
    ext_file = get_vlc_ext_path()
    if os.path.exists(ext_file):
        if messagebox.askokcancel("Remove Extension", "Are you sure you want to remove the VLC extension?", parent=app):
            try:
                os.remove(ext_file)
                messagebox.showinfo("✅ Removed", "The VLC Extension was successfully removed.\n\nPlease completely close and restart VLC Player.", parent=app)
            except Exception as e:
                messagebox.showerror("Error", f"Could not remove extension:\n{e}", parent=app)

        </div>

FILE: .\website\script.js
============================================================

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

    // ── Scroll Animations (Intersection Observer) ──
    const observerOptions = {
        root: null,
        rootMargin: '0px 0px -60px 0px',
        threshold: 0.1
    };

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                entry.target.classList.add('visible');
                observer.unobserve(entry.target);
            }
        });
    }, observerOptions);

    document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));

    // ── Header Scroll Effect ──
    const header = document.getElementById('site-header');
    let lastScroll = 0;

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        const currentScroll = window.scrollY;
        if (currentScroll > 40) {
            header.classList.add('scrolled');
        } else {
            header.classList.remove('scrolled');
        }
        lastScroll = currentScroll;
    }, { passive: true });

    // ── Floating Particles ──
    const particlesContainer = document.getElementById('particles');
    if (particlesContainer) {
        const particleCount = 25;
        for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
            const particle = document.createElement('div');
            particle.classList.add('particle');
            particle.style.left = Math.random() * 100 + '%';
            particle.style.top = (Math.random() * 100 + 100) + '%';
            particle.style.animationDuration = (8 + Math.random() * 15) + 's';
            particle.style.animationDelay = (Math.random() * 10) + 's';
            particle.style.width = (1 + Math.random() * 2) + 'px';
            particle.style.height = particle.style.width;
            particlesContainer.appendChild(particle);
        }
    }

    // ── Mobile Menu Toggle ──
    const mobileBtn = document.getElementById('mobile-menu-btn');
    const mainNav = document.getElementById('main-nav');

    if (mobileBtn && mainNav) {
        mobileBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            mainNav.classList.toggle('mobile-open');
            mobileBtn.classList.toggle('active');
        });
    }

    // ── Smooth Scroll for Anchor Links ──
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(anchor => {
        anchor.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const targetId = anchor.getAttribute('href');
            if (targetId === '#') return;
            const target = document.querySelector(targetId);
            if (target) {
                e.preventDefault();
                target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
                // Close mobile nav if open
                if (mainNav) mainNav.classList.remove('mobile-open');
                if (mobileBtn) mobileBtn.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });
    });

    // ── Subtle Parallax on Hero Showcase ──
    const heroFrame = document.querySelector('.hero-showcase-frame');
    if (heroFrame && window.innerWidth > 960) {
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const x = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 8;
            const y = (e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 8;
            heroFrame.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateY(${x}deg) rotateX(${-y}deg)`;
        }, { passive: true });
    }

});

import os
import warnings
import multiprocessing

if __name__ == '__main__':
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()

warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
os.environ["PYTHONWARNINGS"] = "ignore"

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, ttk, colorchooser
import threading
import subprocess
import sys
import re

import ust_settings as config
from utils import clean_path, format_timestamp, parse_srt, get_available_subtitles, detect_paired_file, generate_ass_content, resource_path, get_ffmpeg_path

# Cleaned up imports (Removed offline references)
from engines import transcribe_audio_chunk, translate_batch, detect_sync_offset
from vlc_integration import launch_vlc_preview, apply_subtitle_to_vlc, install_vlc_extension, remove_vlc_extension, get_vlc_ext_path
from ui_windows import show_srt_preview, show_style_preview, open_history
from security import save_api_key, load_api_key
from reframing import reframe_video

# PIL for thumbnail display (optional — graceful fallback if not installed)
try:
    from PIL import Image, ImageTk
    PIL_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    PIL_AVAILABLE = False

_thumb_img_ref = None  # Prevent garbage collection of the thumbnail PhotoImage

# ============================================================
# HELPER / CALLBACKS
# ============================================================
def update_status(text):
    if app and status_label:
        app.after(0, lambda: status_label.config(text=f"Status: {text}"))

def update_progress(done, total, label=""):
    pct = int((done / total) * 100) if total > 0 else 0
    if app and progress_var:  app.after(0, lambda: progress_var.set(pct))
    if label and app and progress_label:
        app.after(0, lambda: progress_label.config(text=f"{done}/{total} {label}"))

def populate_all_tabs(srt_path):
    app.after(0, lambda: trans_srt_var.set(srt_path))
    app.after(0, lambda: sync_srt_var.set(srt_path))
    app.after(0, lambda: custom_srt_var.set(srt_path))


# ============================================================
# WORKFLOW LOGIC
# ============================================================
def run_generation():
    video_path       = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_lang      = gen_lang_var.get()
    api_key          = api_key_var.get().strip()
    provider         = provider_var.get()
    custom_url       = custom_url_var.get().strip()
    romanize         = gen_romanize_var.get()
    source_lang_name = gen_source_lang_var.get()
    source_lang_code = config.WHISPER_LANGUAGES.get(source_lang_name)

    if not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a video file!")
    if not os.path.exists(video_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Video file not found!")
    if not api_key: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please enter your API Key!")
    save_api_key(api_key)

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: gen_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            if os.path.exists(config.TEMP_DIR):
                for fn in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR):
                    try: os.remove(os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, fn))
                    except: pass

            update_status("Compressing audio for AI (Wait ~15s)...")
            chunk_pattern = os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, "chunk_%03d.m4a")
            cf  = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
            
            ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
            segment_time = str(config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME)
            cmd = [ffmpeg_path, "-y", "-i", video_path, "-vn", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "64k",
                   "-ar", "16000", "-ac", "1", "-af", "loudnorm", "-f", "segment",
                   "-segment_time", segment_time, chunk_pattern]

            try:
                proc = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, creationflags=cf)
                if proc.returncode != 0:
                    raise Exception(f"FFmpeg failed. Is the file a valid video?\n\n{proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')[-400:]}")
            except FileNotFoundError:
                raise Exception("ffmpeg.exe not found! Please make sure the app was installed correctly.")

            chunks = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR) if f.endswith('.m4a')])
            if not chunks:
                raise Exception("No audio chunks extracted — the video may have no audio track.")

            all_segments = []
            for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
                lang_info = f" ({source_lang_name})" if source_lang_code else " (Auto-Detect)"

                # AI Transcription
                update_status(f"☁️ Transcribing Part {i+1}/{len(chunks)}{lang_info} via {provider}...")
                update_progress(i, len(chunks), "parts")
                
                ai_data = transcribe_audio_chunk(
                    os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, chunk), 
                    provider, 
                    api_key, 
                    source_lang_code,
                    custom_url
                )

                offset = i * config.AUDIO_SEGMENT_TIME
                for seg in ai_data.get("segments", []):
                    text = seg["text"].strip()
                    if text:
                        all_segments.append({"start": seg["start"] + offset, "end": seg["end"] + offset, "text": text})

            if not all_segments: raise Exception("No speech detected in this video. Please ensure the video contains spoken words.")

            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            if target_code != "en":
                mode = "Manglish/Hinglish" if romanize else "Native Script"
                update_status(f"🌐 Batch translating {len(all_segments)} lines ({mode})...")
                update_progress(0, 100, "%")
                translate_batch(all_segments, target_code, romanize)
                update_progress(100, 100, "%")

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(all_segments) if s['text'])
            suffix   = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
            srt_name = f"{os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]}{suffix}.srt"
            srt_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, srt_name))

            with open(srt_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
            for fn in os.listdir(config.TEMP_DIR):
                try: os.remove(os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, fn))
                except: pass

            update_status(f"✅ Done! {len(all_segments)} subtitle lines generated.")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(srt_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, srt_path, f"✅ Subtitles Generated!\n{len(all_segments)} lines | {target_lang}", video_path)

        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Generation Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: gen_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()


def run_online_search():
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_lang = gen_lang_var.get()
    
    if not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a video file!")
    if not os.path.exists(video_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Video file not found!")
    
    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: fast_dl_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            from engines import fetch_online_subtitle
            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            out_path = fetch_online_subtitle(video_path, target_code, update_status)
            if out_path:
                populate_all_tabs(out_path)
                apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, out_path, f"✅ Online Subtitle Downloaded!\n{target_lang}", video_path)
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Search Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: fast_dl_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
            
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_translation():
    srt_path    = clean_path(trans_srt_var.get().strip())
    target_lang = trans_lang_var.get()
    romanize    = trans_romanize_var.get()
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Valid SRT file not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: trans_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Parsing SRT file...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            if not segments: raise Exception("Could not parse the SRT file.")

            target_code = config.LANGUAGES.get(target_lang, "en")
            errors = 0
            if target_code != "en":
                mode = "Manglish/Hinglish" if romanize else "Native Script"
                update_status(f"🌐 Batch translating {len(segments)} lines to {target_lang} ({mode})...")
                update_progress(0, 100, "%")
                translate_batch(segments, target_code, romanize)
                update_progress(100, 100, "%")

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(segments))
            base      = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
            suffix    = f"_{target_lang}_Romanized" if romanize else f"_{target_lang}"
            save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}{suffix}.srt"))

            with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)
            note = f"\n⚠️ {errors} errors" if errors else ""
            update_status(f"✅ Translation Done!{note}")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(save_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, save_path, f"✅ Translation Finished!\n{len(segments)} lines → {target_lang}{note}", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Translation Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: trans_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_reframe():
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    target_ratio = reframe_ratio_var.get()
    mode = reframe_mode_var.get()

    if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
        return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please select a valid video file!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: reframe_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED, text="Reframing..."))
            update_status(f"✂️ Reframing video to {target_ratio} ({mode})...")
            update_progress(0, 100, "Reframing")
            
            out_path = reframe_video(video_path, target_ratio, mode)
            
            update_status(f"✅ Reframe Done! Saved to {os.path.basename(out_path)}")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showinfo("Reframing Complete", f"Saved successfully to:\n{out_path}"))
            
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Reframing Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error occurred.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: reframe_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL, text="✂️ Reframe Video"))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_auto_sync():
    srt_path   = clean_path(sync_srt_var.get().strip())
    video_path = clean_path(gen_video_var.get().strip())
    if not srt_path or not video_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Select Video in Tab 1 and an SRT file here!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: auto_sync_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED, text="Analyzing..."))
            offset, info = detect_sync_offset(video_path, srt_path, update_status)
            if offset is None:
                app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Auto-Sync Failed", info))
                update_status("Auto-sync failed.")
                return
            offset_str = f"{offset:+.2f}"
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_offset_var.set(offset_str))
            update_status(f"✅ Auto-detected offset: {offset_str}s")
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showinfo("🎯 Auto-Sync Result", f"Detected Offset: {offset_str}s\n\n{info}\nClick 'SYNC & APPLY' to save!"))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: auto_sync_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL, text="🔍 Auto Detect Offset"))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def run_sync():
    srt_path = clean_path(sync_srt_var.get().strip())
    try: offset_val = float(sync_offset_var.get().strip().replace('+', ''))
    except ValueError: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "Offset must be a number")
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "SRT file not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Adjusting SRT timestamps...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            for seg in segments:
                seg['start'] = max(0, seg['start'] + offset_val)
                seg['end']   = max(0.001, seg['end'] + offset_val)
                if seg['end'] <= seg['start']: seg['end'] = seg['start'] + 0.5

            srt_content = "".join(f"{i+1}\n{format_timestamp(s['start'])} --> {format_timestamp(s['end'])}\n{s['text']}\n\n"
                                  for i, s in enumerate(segments))
            base      = re.sub(r'_synced$', '', os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0])
            save_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_synced.srt"))
            with open(save_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(srt_content)

            update_status(f"✅ Sync done! Offset: {offset_val:+.2f}s")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            populate_all_tabs(save_path)
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, save_path, f"✅ Sync Applied!\nOffset: {offset_val:+.2f} seconds", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Sync Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: sync_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def pick_color(var, btn):
    initial = var.get() if var.get().startswith('#') else '#ffffff'
    result  = colorchooser.askcolor(color=initial, title="Pick Color")
    if result and result[1]:
        var.set(result[1])
        btn.config(bg=result[1], fg="white" if result[1].lower() in ['#000000','#111111','#222222','#333333'] else "black")

def run_export_ass():
    srt_path = clean_path(custom_srt_var.get().strip())
    if not srt_path or not os.path.exists(srt_path): return messagebox.showerror("Error", "File not found!")

    def _task():
        try:
            app.after(0, lambda: export_ass_btn.config(state=tk.DISABLED))
            update_status("Generating styled ASS subtitle...")
            segments = parse_srt(srt_path)
            if not segments: raise Exception("Could not parse SRT file.")

            style = {
                "font_name": custom_font_var.get() or "Arial",
                "font_size": custom_size_var.get(),
                "primary_color": custom_text_color_var.get(),
                "outline_color": custom_outline_color_var.get(),
                "background": custom_bg_var.get(),
                "bold": custom_bold_var.get(),
                "italic": custom_italic_var.get(),
                "outline": custom_outline_sz_var.get(),
                "shadow": custom_shadow_var.get(),
                "alignment": 8 if custom_position_var.get() == "top" else 2,
            }
            ass_content = generate_ass_content(segments, style)
            base        = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
            ass_path    = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_styled.ass"))
            with open(ass_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(ass_content)

            update_status("✅ Styled ASS subtitle exported!")
            update_progress(100, 100, "%")
            
            apply_subtitle_to_vlc(app, ass_path, "✅ Styled Subtitle Exported as .ASS", clean_path(gen_video_var.get()))
        except Exception as e:
            app.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Export Error", str(e)))
            update_status("Error.")
        finally:
            app.after(0, lambda: export_ass_btn.config(state=tk.NORMAL))
    threading.Thread(target=_task, daemon=True).start()

def on_video_change(*args):
    v_path = gen_video_var.get().strip()
    subs   = get_available_subtitles(v_path)
    if trans_srt_cb:  trans_srt_cb['values']  = subs
    if sync_srt_cb:   sync_srt_cb['values']   = subs
    if custom_srt_cb: custom_srt_cb['values'] = subs
    best = detect_paired_file(v_path, "srt")
    if best: populate_all_tabs(best)
    elif subs:
        if not trans_srt_var.get(): populate_all_tabs(subs[0])
    # Launch background thread to extract thumbnail + metadata
    threading.Thread(target=update_video_info, args=(v_path,), daemon=True).start()

def browse_srt(var):
    f = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("Subtitles", "*.srt *.ass")])
    if f: var.set(clean_path(f))

def browse_video(var):
    f = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("Video", "*.mp4 *.mkv *.avi *.ts *.mov *.wmv")])
    if f: var.set(clean_path(f))


# ============================================================
# VIDEO THUMBNAIL + METADATA PANEL
# ============================================================
def update_video_info(video_path):
    """Background thread: extract a thumbnail frame and video metadata, then push to UI."""
    if not video_path or not os.path.exists(video_path):
        app.after(0, _clear_video_info)
        return

    ffmpeg_path = get_ffmpeg_path()
    cf = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0
    os.makedirs(config.TEMP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    thumb_path = os.path.join(config.TEMP_DIR, '_preview_thumb.jpg')

    # --- Extract thumbnail frame at ~3 seconds ---
    try:
        subprocess.run(
            [ffmpeg_path, '-y', '-i', video_path,
             '-ss', '3', '-vframes', '1', '-vf', 'scale=210:-1', thumb_path],
            capture_output=True, creationflags=cf, timeout=20
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    # --- Parse metadata from ffmpeg stderr (no ffprobe needed) ---
    meta = {}
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [ffmpeg_path, '-i', video_path],
            capture_output=True, creationflags=cf, timeout=15
        )
        stderr = result.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')

        # Duration
        dm = re.search(r'Duration:\s*(\d+):(\d+):([\d.]+)', stderr)
        if dm:
            h, m, s = int(dm.group(1)), int(dm.group(2)), float(dm.group(3))
            meta['duration'] = f"{h}h {m}m" if h > 0 else f"{m}m {int(s)}s"

        # Video stream — codec + resolution
        vm = re.search(r'Stream.*?Video:\s*(\w+).*?(\d{3,5})x(\d{3,5})', stderr)
        if vm:
            meta['codec']      = vm.group(1).upper()
            meta['resolution'] = f"{vm.group(2)}\u00d7{vm.group(3)}"

        # FPS
        fps_m = re.search(r'([\d.]+) fps', stderr)
        if fps_m:
            meta['fps'] = fps_m.group(1)

        # Audio codec
        am = re.search(r'Stream.*?Audio:\s*(\w+)', stderr)
        if am:
            meta['audio'] = am.group(1).upper()

        # File size
        size_mb = os.path.getsize(video_path) / (1024 * 1024)
        meta['size'] = f"{size_mb:.1f} MB"

    except Exception:
        pass

    app.after(0, lambda: _update_video_info_ui(thumb_path, meta))


def _clear_video_info():
    """Hide the video info panel."""
    video_info_frame.pack_forget()


def _update_video_info_ui(thumb_path, meta):
    """Runs on main thread: update thumbnail image and metadata text."""
    global _thumb_img_ref

    # --- Thumbnail ---
    resample = getattr(Image, 'LANCZOS', getattr(Image, 'ANTIALIAS', None)) if PIL_AVAILABLE else None
    if PIL_AVAILABLE and os.path.exists(thumb_path) and resample:
        try:
            img = Image.open(thumb_path)
            img.thumbnail((210, 118), resample)
            _thumb_img_ref = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
            thumb_label.config(image=_thumb_img_ref, text='', width=210, height=118)
        except Exception:
            thumb_label.config(image='', text='\U0001f3ac', font=('Segoe UI', 22), width=8, height=4)
            _thumb_img_ref = None
    else:
        thumb_label.config(image='', text='\U0001f3ac', font=('Segoe UI', 22), width=8, height=4)
        _thumb_img_ref = None

    # --- Metadata lines ---
    if meta:
        lines = []
        if 'duration'   in meta: lines.append(f"\u23f1  Duration    {meta['duration']}")
        if 'resolution' in meta: lines.append(f"\U0001f4d0  Resolution  {meta['resolution']}")
        if 'codec' in meta:
            c = meta['codec']
            if 'fps' in meta: c += f"  @  {meta['fps']} fps"
            lines.append(f"\U0001f3ac  Video       {c}")
        if 'audio' in meta: lines.append(f"\U0001f50a  Audio       {meta['audio']}")
        if 'size'  in meta: lines.append(f"\U0001f4be  File Size   {meta['size']}")
        info_label.config(text='\n'.join(lines))
    else:
        info_label.config(text="\u26a0\ufe0f  Could not read metadata.")

    video_info_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=12, pady=(4, 10))

        tk.Radiobutton(R, text=txt, variable=custom_position_var, value=val, bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="white", activebackground=config.PANEL_BG, selectcolor=config.DARK_BG, font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(anchor="w")
    tk.Label(R, text="Outline Size (0–4):", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#aaa", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack(anchor="w", pady=(5, 0))
    tk.Scale(R, from_=0, to=4, orient=tk.HORIZONTAL, variable=custom_outline_sz_var, bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="white", highlightthickness=0, troughcolor="#333", activebackground=config.VLC_ORANGE, length=130).pack(anchor="w")
    tk.Label(R, text="Shadow Size (0–3):", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#aaa", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack(anchor="w")
    tk.Scale(R, from_=0, to=3, orient=tk.HORIZONTAL, variable=custom_shadow_var, bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="white", highlightthickness=0, troughcolor="#333", activebackground=config.VLC_ORANGE, length=130).pack(anchor="w")

    def preview_ass_in_vlc():
        srt_path = clean_path(custom_srt_var.get().strip())
        if not srt_path: return messagebox.showerror("Error", "No file selected!")
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(srt_path))[0]
        ass_path = clean_path(os.path.join(config.SUB_DIR, f"{base}_styled.ass"))
        if os.path.exists(ass_path):
            launch_vlc_preview(clean_path(gen_video_var.get()), ass_path)
        else:
            messagebox.showerror("Not Found", "Please click '🎨 EXPORT' first to generate the styled .ass file before previewing.")

    row_custom = tk.Frame(frame_custom, bg=config.PANEL_BG); row_custom.pack(pady=(8, 3))
    export_ass_btn = tk.Button(row_custom, text="🎨 EXPORT", bg=config.VLC_ORANGE, fg="black", font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold"), relief=tk.FLAT, command=run_export_ass); export_ass_btn.pack(side=tk.LEFT, ipadx=8, ipady=6)
    tk.Button(row_custom, text="👁 SRT", bg="#2a2a2a", fg=config.VLC_ORANGE, relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), padx=6, pady=6, command=lambda: show_srt_preview(app, clean_path(custom_srt_var.get()), "👁 Preview")).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 0))
    tk.Button(row_custom, text="🖥 Style", bg="#1a2a1a", fg="#4CAF50", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), padx=6, pady=6, command=lambda: show_style_preview(app, style_vars)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 0))
    tk.Button(row_custom, text="▶ VLC", bg="#2a2a2a", fg="#4CAF50", relief=tk.FLAT, font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), padx=6, pady=6, command=preview_ass_in_vlc).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(3, 0))
    tk.Label(frame_custom, text="ASS format = full styling in VLC", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#555", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack()

    # Tab 5
    tk.Label(frame_reframe, text="Select Video for Reframing:", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="white", font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(pady=(12, 2))
    fr1 = tk.Frame(frame_reframe, bg=config.PANEL_BG); fr1.pack()
    reframe_video_cb = ttk.Combobox(fr1, textvariable=gen_video_var, state="normal", width=42); reframe_video_cb.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
    tk.Button(fr1, text="Browse", command=lambda: browse_video(gen_video_var), bg="#333", fg="white", relief=tk.FLAT).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    
    fr2 = tk.Frame(frame_reframe, bg=config.PANEL_BG); fr2.pack(pady=10)
    tk.Label(fr2, text="Target Ratio:", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#ccc", font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 5))
    ttk.Combobox(fr2, textvariable=reframe_ratio_var, values=["16:9", "9:16", "1:1", "4:5", "3:4", "21:9"], state="readonly", width=10).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 15))
    
    tk.Label(fr2, text="Mode:", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#ccc", font=("Segoe UI", 9)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 5))
    ttk.Combobox(fr2, textvariable=reframe_mode_var, values=["Blurred Background (Professional)", "Center Crop (Fill)"], state="readonly", width=32).pack(side=tk.LEFT)
    
    tk.Label(frame_reframe, text="Perfect for YouTube Shorts, TikTok, and Instagram Reels", bg=config.PANEL_BG, fg="#777", font=("Segoe UI", 8)).pack(pady=(0, 10))
    reframe_btn = tk.Button(frame_reframe, text="✂️ Reframe Video", bg=config.VLC_ORANGE, fg="black", font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold"), relief=tk.FLAT, command=run_reframe)
    reframe_btn.pack(pady=5, ipadx=10, ipady=4)

    bot = tk.Frame(app, bg=config.DARK_BG); bot.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=20, pady=(0, 10))
    ttk.Progressbar(bot, variable=progress_var, maximum=100, length=520, mode='determinate', style="Orange.Horizontal.TProgressbar").pack()
    progress_label = tk.Label(bot, text="", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg=config.VLC_ORANGE, font=("Segoe UI", 9)); progress_label.pack()
    status_label = tk.Label(bot, text="Status: Ready", bg=config.DARK_BG, fg="#888", font=("Segoe UI", 9)); status_label.pack()

    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        passed = sys.argv[1]

        if passed.startswith("universalsubtitles://") or (len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[1] == "--uri"):
            from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs, unquote
            uri = passed if passed.startswith("universalsubtitles://") else sys.argv[2]
            uri = uri.strip('"').rstrip('/')
            parsed = urlparse(uri)
            params = parse_qs(parsed.query)
            video = unquote(params.get("video", [""])[0])
            if video and os.path.isfile(video):
                gen_video_var.set(clean_path(video))
        else:
            passed = clean_path(passed)
            if os.path.isfile(passed):
                if passed.lower().endswith('.srt'):
                    populate_all_tabs(passed)
                    vm = detect_paired_file(passed, "video")
                    if vm: gen_video_var.set(vm)
                else: gen_video_var.set(passed)

    show_tab(0)
    app.mainloop()

import os
import sys

# Ensure VLC DLLs can be found before importing anything that imports vlc
vlc_dir = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'vlc_64')
if os.path.exists(vlc_dir):
    os.environ['PYTHON_VLC_MODULE_PATH'] = vlc_dir
    if hasattr(os, 'add_dll_directory'):
        os.add_dll_directory(vlc_dir)

import threading
import subprocess
import time
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QApplication, QMainWindow, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, 
                               QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, 
                               QLineEdit, QCheckBox, QStackedWidget, QProgressBar,
                               QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QFrame, QScrollArea, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QSlider)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, Signal, QObject, QMimeData, QUrl
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon, QFont, QColor, QDrag

class HistoryListWidget(QListWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setDragEnabled(True)

    def startDrag(self, supportedActions):
        item = self.currentItem()
        if not item: return
        path = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        if not path or not os.path.exists(path): return
        
        mime_data = QMimeData()
        mime_data.setUrls([QUrl.fromLocalFile(path)])
        drag = QDrag(self)
        drag.setMimeData(mime_data)
        drag.exec(supportedActions)

# A signal emitter for thread-safe UI updates
class WorkerSignals(QObject):
    update_status = Signal(str)
    update_progress = Signal(int, int, str)
    error = Signal(str)
    finished = Signal()
    populate_tabs = Signal(str)
    sync_offset_detected = Signal(str, str)


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("Universal Subtitles (.vsub) Generate")
        self.resize(1200, 800)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QMainWindow { background-color: #111111; }
            QLabel { color: #ffffff; }
            QPushButton { 
                background-color: #2a2a2a; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; padding: 6px 12px; font-weight: bold;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #333333; }
            QPushButton.primary { background-color: #ff9900; color: black; }
            QPushButton.primary:hover { background-color: #e68a00; }
            QPushButton.secondary { background-color: #008cba; color: white; }
            QComboBox, QLineEdit { 
                background-color: #222222; color: white; border: 1px solid #333; border-radius: 4px; padding: 4px;
            }
            QFrame.sidebar { background-color: #1a1a1a; }
            QFrame.panel { background-color: #1a1a1a; border-radius: 8px; }
        """)

        self.signals = WorkerSignals()
        self.signals.update_status.connect(self.set_status)
        self.signals.update_progress.connect(self.set_progress)
        self.signals.error.connect(self.show_error)
        self.signals.populate_tabs.connect(self.populate_all_tabs)
        self.signals.sync_offset_detected.connect(self.on_sync_offset_detected)

        self.init_ui()

        # Update timer for timeline
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_timeline)
        self.timer.start(100) # 100ms updates
